# Chinese official statements and records, 1933-1987

Statements of the ROC and the PRC on the City, printed at the time, and the 2007 selection of archive documents.

- **Status:** read: 29 of 29 items read by the reading of the source search of 2026-11.
  A checker checked a sample of the facts. Each fact gives its page and a short exact quote.
- **Dates:** 1928-1987.
- **Items:** 29, of which 29 are open now. Value of the set: high.
- **Copyright:** see the licence of each item. Copyrighted files go in `local/`, which git ignores.
- **Stored in git:** this file only.

## Why this is primary

Each item says its class and the reason. An item that is "retrospective" is first-hand
testimony given later: tag its facts "retrospective (year)".

## Items

### china-1: 外交部新闻发言人发表谈话 对港英清拆九龙城寨表示理解

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-18; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-20.
- **Citation:** Xinhua News Agency (新華社), Beijing, 14 Jan 1987: 「外交部新闻发言人发表谈话 对港英清拆九龙城寨表示理解」, People's Daily (人民日報), 15 Jan 1987, p. 4. Read on the 老資料網 mirror: https://www.laoziliao.net/rmrb/1987-01-15-4#750554
- **Class:** primary (An official statement of the PRC Foreign Ministry, printed the next day. A record made at the time.)
- **Date of the record:** 1987-01-14 (statement and Xinhua dispatch); printed 1987-01-15

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| People's Daily (人民日報), 15 Jan 1987, page 4 (第4版 政治·法律·社会), item anchor #750554 | On 14 Jan 1987 the spokesman of the PRC Foreign Ministry gave a statement on Kowloon Walled City (九龍城寨). The spokesman is not named. | "新华社北京1月14日电 外交部新闻发言人今天发表谈话" (Xinhua, Beijing, 14 Jan: the Foreign Ministry spokesman today gave a statement.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 15 Jan 1987, page 4 (第4版 政治·法律·社会), item anchor #750554 | China said that it 'fully understands' the decision of the British Hong Kong government to clear the City and to build a park on the site. | "对香港英国政府准备清拆九龙城寨，并在原址上兴建公园的决定表示充分理解" (expressed full understanding of the decision of the British Hong Kong government to clear Kowloon Walled City and build a park on the original site) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 15 Jan 1987, page 4 (第4版 政治·法律·社会), item anchor #750554 | The statement called the City, like the rest of Hong Kong, a problem left over from history, but with a special historical background. | "九龙城寨和香港其他地区一样是历史遗留下来的问题，但有其特殊的历史背景" (Kowloon Walled City, like other parts of Hong Kong, is a problem left over from history, but it has a special historical background.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 15 Jan 1987, page 4 (第4版 政治·法律·社会), item anchor #750554 | The statement said that the Joint Declaration of 19 Dec 1984 made the conditions to improve the living environment of the City's residents at the root, as early as possible. | "为尽早从根本上改善九龙城寨居民的生活环境创造了条件" (created the conditions to improve, as early as possible and at the root, the living environment of the residents of Kowloon Walled City) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 15 Jan 1987, page 4 (第4版 政治·法律·社会), item anchor #750554 | The statement said that a real improvement of the City's living environment was in the interest of the City residents and of all Hong Kong residents. | "不但符合城寨居民的切身利益，也符合全体香港居民的利益" (is not only in the vital interest of the City residents but also in the interest of all Hong Kong residents) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 15 Jan 1987, page 4 (第4版 政治·法律·社会), item anchor #750554 | The statement gave the 'prosperity and stability' of all Hong Kong as its reason for the understanding, and called the planned measures 'proper measures'. | "从整个香港的繁荣与稳定出发，我们对于香港英国政府准备采取妥善措施，清拆九龙城寨" (proceeding from the prosperity and stability of the whole of Hong Kong, [we understand] that the British Hong Kong government will take proper measures to clear Kowloon Walled City) |

- **Leads:**
  - Sino-British Joint Declaration on the Question of Hong Kong, signed 19 Dec 1984 (cited by the statement).
### top-china-1: 大公報 1987-01-15

- **Status:** Partly read on 2026-11-18; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-20. An earlier run of this chunk fetched the full-size page images (leaves n0, n1, n4, n6) on 2026-11-18; on 2026-11-20 I read them as cropped, enlarged images. The OCR is horizontal OCR of vertical text and is not usable. I read the City articles of the front page (leaf n0), the Xinhua Hong Kong Branch questions and answers (leaf n1), the City page (leaf n4) and the photo caption on leaf n6. Not read in full: the front-page lead on the Beijing reaction (its Foreign Ministry text is the same as china-1), the rest of the government spokesman's questions and answers and the 馮檢基 article on leaf n4 (only their first parts), and the photos. Some characters are under stains or folds; I mark those readings.
- **Citation:** Ta Kung Pao (大公報), Hong Kong, 15 Jan 1987, leaves n0, n1, n4 and n6 (front page, Xinhua branch interview, City news page, photo page). Internet Archive item NPTKP19870115, https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19870115
- **Class:** primary (A Hong Kong newspaper of the day after the clearance announcement of 14 Jan 1987. Ta Kung Pao was a pro-Beijing paper.)
- **Date of the record:** 1987-01-15
- **Files:**
  - `top-china-1-NPTKP19870115-b507bbb7.pdf` (in `local/`, not in git): Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, whole issue, page-image PDF (14,075,574 bytes); City items on leaves n0, n1, n4 and n6; https://archive.org/download/NPTKP19870115/NPTKP19870115.pdf; 14075574 bytes; SHA-256 `4d4e4f9a91aad9fd3ee7bd93228b30a48f693895f435fef14fadeb394844bf99`; licence: no rights statement on the item (© 大公報) (https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19870115)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n0, 「清拆將分三期進行 計劃三年全部完工」 | The Hong Kong government announced at 9 am on 14 Jan 1987 its plan to clear the City and to make it a park within three years. | "港府是於昨日上午九時宣佈該項清拆九龍城砦，改善環境計劃的" (The government announced the plan to clear Kowloon Walled City and improve the environment at 9 am yesterday.) |
| Ta Kung Pao (大公報), 15 Jan 1987, archive.org NPTKP19870115, leaf n0 (front page), 「房署三百職員入城寨工作 五千餘家庭已登記 當局將予安置補償」 | At once 360 Housing Department staff started the first registration of the residents. (The headline says 300.) | "三百六十名房屋署職員隨即替當地居民辦理初步登記工作" (360 Housing Department staff at once did the preliminary registration of the local residents) |
| Ta Kung Pao (大公報), 15 Jan 1987, archive.org NPTKP19870115, leaf n0 (front page), 「房署三百職員入城寨工作 五千餘家庭已登記 當局將予安置補償」 | By the end of 14 Jan 1987 the survey had registered 8,788 living units, 5,116 households and 19,606 persons. | "已登記的居住單位共八千七百八十八個，已登記的家庭有五千一百一十六戶，登記人數則為一萬九千六百零六人" (registered living units 8,788; registered households 5,116; registered persons 19,606) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n0, 「登記工作周內完成」 | On the first day 3,650 living units and 56 commercial units were locked with nobody at home, and 23 households refused to register (the box on leaf n4 gives 22). | "共有三千六百五十個居住單位和五十六個商業單位，因戶主外出而大門深鎖，以及有二十三個住戶拒絕接受登記" (3,650 living units and 56 commercial units were locked because the occupants were out, and 23 households refused registration) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n0, 「登記工作周內完成」 | The survey teams carried paint and blackboards. At each home or shop they painted a number on the wall, and recorded the number of persons and the floor area, and took photographs. | "每到一戶民居或商戶，則用油漆在牆壁上註下號碼，並登記人數、面積以及拍攝作記錄" (at each dwelling or shop they marked a number on the wall in paint, recorded the number of people and the area, and photographed it) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n0, 「登記工作周內完成」 | The residents were surprised but did not panic, and the registration went without trouble. | "城砦內的居民對於他們的行動雖感到突然，但也沒有慌亂，登記工作進程中並沒有不愉快事件發生" (The City residents found it sudden but did not panic; there was no unpleasant incident during registration.) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n0, 「清拆將分三期進行 計劃三年全部完工」 | The government spokesman listed the problems of the City: flooding, dangerous buildings, poor sanitation and a serious fire danger. | "包括水浸、危險建築物、衛生情況惡劣及火警危險嚴重等" (including flooding, dangerous buildings, bad sanitary conditions and serious fire danger) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n0, 「清拆將分三期進行 計劃三年全部完工」 | The clearance was to be done in three phases over three years and to be finished by Mar 1990. No new buildings were to be built; the site was to become a park, perhaps with some community facilities. | "到一九九〇年三月應全部完成。該區清拆後將不再興建樓宇，但會發展成為一個公園" (should be fully completed by March 1990. After clearance no more buildings will be built; it will become a park) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「港府發言人答覆記者詢問稱 城砦清拆改建為公園改善環境合港人利益」 (question and answer) | INFERRED: The spokesman said that the plan could cost several billion dollars, to be paid by the Hong Kong government. | "清拆計劃可能需要[數]十億元，而這筆費用將由港府承擔" (the clearance plan may need several billion dollars, and the government will bear the cost) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n0, 「清拆將分三期進行 計劃三年全部完工」 | The survey was to fix who the real residents were; later the government would decide who could get rehousing and compensation. The spokesman said that all eligible people would get suitable rehousing and compensation. | "確定誰是該處的真正居民，日後並決定那些人有資格獲得安置及補償" (to determine who are the genuine residents there, and later decide who is eligible for rehousing and compensation) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n0, 「登記工作周內完成」 | The officer in charge of the survey was 陳德平, Assistant Director of Housing (Special Duties) (房屋署助理署長(專責事務)). He said that the Housing Authority would meet on 15 Jan 1987 to set up a special committee on clearance, compensation and rehousing. | "房屋委員會今日將舉行特別會議，研究成立一個特別委員會來解決九龍城砦清拆的問題" (The Housing Authority will hold a special meeting today to study setting up a special committee to deal with the clearance of the City.) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n0, 「登記工作周內完成」 | 陳德平 said that most residents cooperated, welcomed the clearance and were not happy with their present living conditions. | "九龍城砦的大部分居民對昨日執行登記行動的房署職員給予充分合作" (most residents of the City cooperated fully with the Housing Department staff who did the registration yesterday) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n0, 「登記工作周內完成」 | INFERRED: The rehousing arrangements were to follow the standards of urban redevelopment clearances. | "[安]置安排將根據市區重建清拆的標準去決定" (the rehousing arrangements will be decided according to the standards of urban redevelopment clearance) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n0, 「登記工作周內完成」 | The deputy secretary 呂孝端 (副政務司(專責事務)) said that the registration should finish within the week. Residents not yet registered could go from the next week to a long-term office in Block 17 of Lok Fu Estate (樂富邨十七座), open until the clearance ended. | "前往樂富邨十七座的一個長期辦事處辦理登記手續" (go to a long-term office at Block 17, Lok Fu Estate to register) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n1, 「新華社香港分社答記者談城寨事」 | The Xinhua News Agency Hong Kong Branch spokesman said that China fully understood the decision, and spoke of improving the living environment of 'more than 40,000' City residents. | "盡早採取妥善措施改善九龍城寨四萬多居民的生活環境" (take proper measures as early as possible to improve the living environment of the more than 40,000 residents of Kowloon Walled City) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n1, 「新華社香港分社答記者談城寨事」 | Asked if the matter was discussed in the Joint Liaison Group, the Xinhua spokesman said that the two sides had useful talks 'through different channels'. | "中英雙方就這個問題曾通過不同的渠道進行過有益的討論" (China and Britain had useful discussions on this question through different channels) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n1, 「新華社香港分社答記者談城寨事」 | The Xinhua spokesman did not comment on compensation and rehousing; he said that the authorities would decide after they asked the people concerned. | "相信有關當局會根據實際情況徵求有關人士的意見作出規定，我不便於在此先作評論" (I believe the authorities will consult the people concerned and decide; it is not convenient for me to comment now) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4 (City page), 「衞生環境非常惡劣 城砦清拆實屬好事 區內居民表示歡迎 希望港府盡快宣佈賠償安置辦法」 | City residents interviewed said that the clearance was sudden but on the whole a good thing because the environment was very bad. They asked for the compensation and rehousing terms soon, mainly owners and shop owners. | "他們認為城砦的環境問題非常惡劣，清拆亦不失為一件好事" (they consider the City's environmental problems very bad, and clearance is not a bad thing) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4 (City page), 「衞生環境非常惡劣 城砦清拆實屬好事 區內居民表示歡迎 希望港府盡快宣佈賠償安置辦法」 | Most residents did not know of the announcement until Housing Department staff came to register them. Many left their work and gathered in the narrow lanes to talk about it; more than 100 went to the office of the Kai Fong Association. | "成羣的聚集在狹窄的巷道上，就清拆事件議論紛紛。一百多名居民亦走到街坊福利會辦事處" (gathered in groups in the narrow lanes discussing the clearance; more than a hundred residents also went to the Kai Fong Association office) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4 (City page), 「衞生環境非常惡劣 城砦清拆實屬好事 區內居民表示歡迎 希望港府盡快宣佈賠償安置辦法」 | A dessert shop opened on 5 Jan 1987. Its owner had borrowed HK$800,000 from relatives and friends, paid HK$500,000 for the shop unit and spent the rest on fitting it out. He had hoped to get his money back in two or three years. | "籌集了八十萬元，用五十萬買下目前的舖位，其餘用在裝修等方面" (raised 800,000 dollars, used 500,000 to buy the present shop unit, and the rest on renovation etc.) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4 (City page), 「衞生環境非常惡劣 城砦清拆實屬好事 區內居民表示歡迎 希望港府盡快宣佈賠償安置辦法」 | 李太太, who had lived in the City for more than 30 years, welcomed the clearance: the air was bad, summer was unbearably hot, and the drinking water supply was poor. | "空氣太差了，夏天更是熱不可耐，加上食水供應情況甚差" (the air is too bad, summer is unbearably hot, and the drinking water supply is very poor) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4 (City page), 「衞生環境非常惡劣 城砦清拆實屬好事 區內居民表示歡迎 希望港府盡快宣佈賠償安置辦法」 | An old resident, 楊伯, said that the residents would gladly leave if the compensation was satisfactory and the rehousing was nearby. | "只要賠償幅度令人滿意，又可就近安置，城砦居民是樂意遷出的" (as long as the compensation is satisfactory and rehousing is nearby, City residents are happy to move out) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「街坊福利會發聲明稱照顧到坊眾利益 盼當局認眞處理善後問題」 | INFERRED: The Kai Fong Association (九龍城砦街坊福利事業促進委員會) held an emergency meeting and issued a statement. It said that the policy looked after the residents' interests and had China's approval, and was 'a good thing'. | "這項政策有[照顧]到城砦坊眾的利[益]，並得到中國政府[認]可，這是恰當的" (this policy takes care of the City residents' interests and has the Chinese government's approval; it is proper) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「街坊福利會發聲明稱照顧到坊眾利益 盼當局認眞處理善後問題」 | INFERRED: The committee's chairman (李偉 plus one character I cannot read) passed on the views of the City's 'medical workers': the plan said nothing on the method of compensation or the place of rehousing. They asked that their medical equipment be counted in the compensation and for a living allowance to change trade. | "能將他們的醫療儀器計算在內，並希望當局發給生活補助費協助他們轉業" (that their medical equipment be included, and that the authorities give a living allowance to help them change trade) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「街坊福利會發聲明稱照顧到坊眾利益 盼當局認眞處理善後問題」 | The chairman said that the 'medical workers' in the City were too many to count, and that there were more than 90 dentists' practices. | "在城砦行醫的「醫務人員」人數很多，很難作出估計，牙醫方面則估計有九十多家" (the 'medical workers' practising in the City are many and hard to estimate; dentists are estimated at more than ninety) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4 (City page), 「衞生環境非常惡劣 城砦清拆實屬好事 區內居民表示歡迎 希望港府盡快宣佈賠償安置辦法」 | Some unlicensed dentists said that they could not easily change trade and felt lost. The association asked that the unlicensed dentists be allowed to practise in other districts. | "他希望港府能容許城砦的無牌牙醫繼續在其他地區經營" (he hopes the government will allow the City's unlicensed dentists to continue in other districts) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, box 「城砦人口統計數字」 | The full survey counts of 14 Jan 1987 (box): 8,788 living units registered; 5,116 households; 19,606 persons; 3,650 units locked with nobody in; 22 households present that refused; 760 commercial units surveyed; 56 commercial units locked. | "已調查的商業單位：七百六十個" (commercial units surveyed: 760) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「清拆行動及善後問題 中英政府意見均相同 鍾逸傑表示牙醫問題會適當安排」 | The Acting Governor, Sir David Akers-Jones (鍾逸傑), called the City 'very old and dirty' and said that China and Britain had discussed the matter on and off for several months. | "我們曾就此事斷斷續續討論數月" (we discussed this matter on and off for several months) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「清拆行動及善後問題 中英政府意見均相同 鍾逸傑表示牙醫問題會適當安排」 | INFERRED: 鍾逸傑 said that the residents were not asked before the survey because news could leak and swindlers could use it to get housing and compensation. | "可能會有騙徒藉此取得房屋及賠償" (swindlers might use this to obtain housing and compensation) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「清拆行動及善後問題 中英政府意見均相同 鍾逸傑表示牙醫問題會適當安排」 | 鍾逸傑 said that the 40,000 residents of the City were all Hong Kong people and must be rehoused, and that the cost would be very large. | "城砦中四萬名居民均為香港人，他們居於此，便需為他們安排住屋" (the 40,000 residents of the City are all Hong Kong people; they live here, so housing must be arranged for them) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「清拆行動及善後問題 中英政府意見均相同 鍾逸傑表示牙醫問題會適當安排」 | 鍾逸傑 said that a park was chosen because the site is very near the airport, unfit for high-density building, and Kowloon City is very dense. | "由於這裏非常接近機場，若發展高密度屋宇實在不適宜" (because it is very near the airport, high-density housing would not be suitable) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「清拆行動及善後問題 中英政府意見均相同 鍾逸傑表示牙醫問題會適當安排」 | 鍾逸傑 said that he did not believe many illegal immigrants lived in the City. | "他不相信城砦內有很多的非法入境者" (he does not believe there are many illegal immigrants in the City) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「清拆行動及善後問題 中英政府意見均相同 鍾逸傑表示牙醫問題會適當安排」 | 鍾逸傑 said that illegal dentists had been found in all clearances of the past 20 years, and would not be a special problem. He called the City a long-standing 'cancer' (毒瘤) of Kowloon. | "我們正除去這個長久以來在九龍令人憂慮的毒瘤" (we are removing this long-standing worrying cancer in Kowloon) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「港府發言人答覆記者詢問稱 城砦清拆改建為公園改善環境合港人利益」 (question and answer) | The government spokesman said that the City lies directly under the flight path, thus for air safety and town planning no buildings were to be built on the site. Part of Carpenter Road (賈炳達道) was already a park. | "城砦直接位於飛機航道下" (the City lies directly under the flight path) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「兩局議員區議員等歡迎此次清拆計劃」 | INFERRED: A Housing Authority member (name under a stain) estimated that more than 40,000 City residents were to be rehoused over three years. | "分三年安置城砦四萬多名居民" (rehouse the more than 40,000 City residents over three years) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「房署人員突登記 城砦居民感錯愕 街道彷如八陣圖 工作人員頭亦暈」 | The Housing Department staff, though each had a work area, were confused by the door numbers and needed residents to show them the right places. | "仍被門牌號碼弄得暈頭轉向，要街坊指點，才能找出正確位置" (were still confused by the door numbers and needed neighbours to point out the right location) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「房署人員突登記 城砦居民感錯愕 街道彷如八陣圖 工作人員頭亦暈」 | The City had small factories of every trade that were outside government control, thus with lower costs. Their main worry was whether they would get rehoused. | "城砦之內有各類小型工廠，經營各行各業，過去他們都不受港府管制，成本因而減少" (the City had all kinds of small factories in every trade; they were not under government control, so costs were lower) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4, 「房署人員突登記 城砦居民感錯愕 街道彷如八陣圖 工作人員頭亦暈」 | The City had five or six estate agencies that dealt only in property inside the City. 陳先生, owner of one on Tung Tsing Road (東正道), said that after the news many more owners came to sell, but there were no buyers, and some buyers who had paid deposits gave them up. | "城砦內有五、六間地產公司，專營區內的物業買賣生意" (the City had five or six estate agencies doing property sales in the area) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n6 (photo page), photo caption | A photo caption said that the City's residents got all their drinking water from street standpipes. | "城砦居民食水，全靠街喉供應" (City residents' drinking water depends wholly on street standpipes) |

- **Leads:**
  - Housing Authority special meeting of 15 Jan 1987 on a special committee for the City clearance (announced).
  - Housing Department survey notices of 14 Jan 1987 and its Special Duties office at Block 17, Lok Fu Estate (named).
  - Statement of the 九龍城砦街坊福利事業促進委員會, 14 Jan 1987 (quoted in part).

| Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 1947-12-17, headline list in the archive.org description of item NPWK19471217 | The Kowloon court ordered the wooden huts in the City pulled down, and a site was prepared for an exchange. | "九龍法庭下令拆毀龍城木屋準備地點交換" (the Kowloon court ordered the demolition of the wooden huts of Lung Shing, and prepared a site for exchange) |

| Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 1947-12-30, headline list in the archive.org description of item NPWK19471230 | On 29 Dec 1947 the residents got a new notice: 71 houses with 48 households must be pulled down. | "龍城七十一間民房四十八家必須遷拆" (in Lung Shing seventy-one houses, forty-eight households, must move and be pulled down) |
| Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 1948-01-10, headline list in the archive.org description of item NPWK19480110 | After the demolition of 5 Jan 1948 the residents said they wanted rice, not gifts of fruit and cakes. | "九龍城同胞要米不須贈菓餅之類" (the compatriots of Kowloon City want rice, not gifts of fruit, cakes and the like) |

| Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 1948-01-14, headline list in the archive.org description of item NPWK19480114 | The evicted residents raised a flag and said they would stay. They slept on the ground with blankets as their roof. | "以地為榻以氈被為屋蓋" (with the ground as their bed and blankets as their roof) |

| Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 1948-01-18, headline list in the archive.org description of item NPWK19480118 | INFERRED: A count of 18 Jan 1948: the City had 135 households, 115 households had been pulled down, 797 people had registered and about 400 had not. | "龍城一三五戶已拆一一五戶已登記七九七人未登記約四百人" (Lung Shing: 135 households, 115 households already pulled down, 797 people registered, about 400 not registered) |

| Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 1948-01-21, headline list in the archive.org description of item NPWK19480121 | The Hong Kong authorities formally denied that they had set a deadline for the City residents to move. | "港當局正式否認限龍城居民遷出" (the Hong Kong authorities formally deny setting a time limit for the Lung Shing residents to move out) |
| Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 1948-01-23, headline list in the archive.org description of item NPWK19480123 | In a census of the City in January 1948 the residents had to be photographed. | "龍城查戶口居民須攝影" (in the Lung Shing household census the residents must be photographed) |
| Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 1948-01-24, headline list in the archive.org description of item NPWK19480124 | The residents' association built an office in the City; 張忠武 worked there with his wound. | "龍城居聯蓋辦事處張忠武氏帶傷工作" (the Lung Shing residents' association builds an office; Mr 張忠武 works while wounded) |
| Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 1948-01-30, headline list in the archive.org description of item NPWK19480130 | A rice shop gave more than 200 catties of rice, and the residents' association gave out relief rice. | "龍城居民會發振米米店捐米二百餘斤" (the Lung Shing residents' association issues relief rice; a rice shop donates over 200 catties of rice) |

| Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 1948-02-05, headline list in the archive.org description of item NPWK19480205 | The number of police stationed in the walled City (九龍寨城) was reduced in early February 1948. | "九龍寨城駐警減少" (the police stationed in Kowloon walled City are reduced) |
| Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 1948-02-07, headline list in the archive.org description of item NPWK19480207 | The wounded resident 賴鳳英 had recovered by 7 Feb 1948. | "九龍城受傷居民賴鳳英痊愈" (the wounded Kowloon City resident 賴鳳英 has recovered) |
| Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 1948-02-12, headline list in the archive.org description of item NPWK19480212 | INFERRED: A headline of 12 Feb 1948 tells of a man named 廖雲彬: his factory closed and he lost his work; his wife bore twin boys and died of a stroke; he asked someone to adopt the weak sons. | "廖雲彬工廠關門失業老妻一產兩雄中風死兩子瘦弱失母求收養" (廖雲彬: factory closed, out of work; wife bore twin boys, died of a stroke; the two thin sons have lost their mother, adoption sought) |
| Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 1948-02-18, headline list in the archive.org description of item NPWK19480218 | The losses of the City residents were registered on 18 Feb 1948, and false claims were strictly forbidden. | "龍城居民損失今日豋記嚴禁虛報" (the losses of the Lung Shing residents are registered today; false reports are strictly forbidden) |

### china-9: 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-18; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-20.
- **Citation:** 國民黨駐港澳總支部 (Kuomintang Hong Kong and Macau General Branch), 《國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊》, Hong Kong, 1 Jan 1948; article 唐飄, 「港九澳一年來大事記」. Transcription: https://zh.wikisource.org/wiki/%E5%9C%8B%E6%B0%91%E9%BB%A8%E9%A7%90%E6%B8%AF%E6%BE%B3%E7%B8%BD%E6%94%AF%E9%83%A8%E5%85%83%E6%97%A6%E7%89%B9%E5%88%8A ; scan: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:SSID-13689353_%E5%9C%8B%E6%B0%91%E9%BB%A8%E9%A7%90%E6%B8%AF%E6%BE%B3%E7%B8%BD%E6%94%AF%E9%83%A8%E5%85%83%E6%97%A6%E7%89%B9%E5%88%8A.pdf
- **Class:** primary (A party yearbook chronicle written within weeks of the events of Nov-Dec 1947. A record made at the time, from the ROC (Kuomintang) side; it is a party, not a government, source.)
- **Date of the record:** 1948-01-01 (publication); it records events of 27 Nov to 12 Dec 1947
- **Files:**
  - `china-9-SSID-13689353__-cd350e63.pdf` (in `local/`, not in git): Scan of the whole 1948 New Year special issue (PDF, 21,751,075 bytes); the City chronicle is in section 港九澳一年來大事記 (open licence, but kept in local/ because of its size: the host keeps the open copy); https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/7/7e/SSID-13689353_%E5%9C%8B%E6%B0%91%E9%BB%A8%E9%A7%90%E6%B8%AF%E6%BE%B3%E7%B8%BD%E6%94%AF%E9%83%A8%E5%85%83%E6%97%A6%E7%89%B9%E5%88%8A.pdf; 21751075 bytes; SHA-256 `af9420dbaefd8fd1bce1d4ddb312fb758051fa49ee281a62308fd4c51b3a02ae`; licence: Public domain (Commons file page, LicenseShortName 'Public domain') (https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:SSID-13689353_%E5%9C%8B%E6%B0%91%E9%BB%A8%E9%A7%90%E6%B8%AF%E6%BE%B3%E7%B8%BD%E6%94%AF%E9%83%A8%E5%85%83%E6%97%A6%E7%89%B9%E5%88%8A.pdf)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊 (1 Jan 1948), section 港九澳一年來大事記 (by 唐飄) › 甲、港九方面 › 二、九龍居民禍不單行 (Chinese Wikisource transcription) | On 27 Nov 1947 the Hong Kong government served a notice on the residents of Kowloon City (九龍城) to move out within two weeks. The Public Works Department notice set the limit at 10 Dec 1947. | "於十一月廿七日送達通告，限令九龍城居民於兩星期內遷出，（查港府工務局通告，限令至十二月十日止）" (On 27 Nov a notice was served ordering Kowloon City residents to move out within two weeks (the Public Works Department notice set the limit at 10 Dec).) |
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊 (1 Jan 1948), section 港九澳一年來大事記 (by 唐飄) › 甲、港九方面 › 二、九龍居民禍不單行 (Chinese Wikisource transcription) | The chronicle gave the government's aim as tidying the look of the City and confirming its rule over the City. | "港政府當局爲整飾九龍城市容，確證對九龍城的轄治" (The Hong Kong government, to tidy the appearance of Kowloon City and confirm its rule over it) |
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊 (1 Jan 1948), section 港九澳一年來大事記 (by 唐飄) › 甲、港九方面 › 二、九龍居民禍不單行 (Chinese Wikisource transcription) | The residents formed a joint assembly named '「寶安縣九龍城」居民聯合大會' and sent express telegrams asking the Chinese government to protest. | "因成立「寶安縣九龍城」居民聯合大會，並發出快郵代電請我政府迅予提出嚴重抗議" (thus they set up the 「寶安縣九龍城」 residents' joint assembly and sent express telegrams asking our government to protest strongly) |
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊 (1 Jan 1948), section 港九澳一年來大事記 (by 唐飄) › 甲、港九方面 › 二、九龍居民禍不單行 (Chinese Wikisource transcription) | On 4 Dec 1947 the Foreign Ministry's special commissioner for Guangdong and Guangxi, 郭德華, flew to Shanghai and on to Nanjing to get instructions. | "十二月四日兩廣外交特派員郭德華，特飛滬轉京，向我外交部請示處理的辦法" (On 4 Dec the Liangguang foreign affairs commissioner 郭德華 flew to Shanghai and on to Nanjing to ask the Foreign Ministry how to handle it.) |
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊 (1 Jan 1948), section 港九澳一年來大事記 (by 唐飄) › 甲、港九方面 › 二、九龍居民禍不單行 (Chinese Wikisource transcription) | Before he left, 郭德華 met the Governor, 葛量洪爵士. The Governor answered that the action was unavoidable and not his own wish. | "港督答稱：這實不得已的事，誠非其本人所願" (The Governor answered: this is truly unavoidable, and really not his own wish.) |
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊 (1 Jan 1948), section 港九澳一年來大事記 (by 唐飄) › 甲、港九方面 › 二、九龍居民禍不單行 (Chinese Wikisource transcription) | On 6 Dec 1947 the residents petitioned the Guangdong Provincial Assembly, and their association held a press conference. They said that they would fight to the death. | "十二月六日並去呈廣東省參議會呼籲，詳述經過情形。並聯會招待新聞界，表示誓死力爭" (On 6 Dec they petitioned the Guangdong Provincial Assembly ... and the association received the press, vowing to fight to the death.) |
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊 (1 Jan 1948), section 港九澳一年來大事記 (by 唐飄) › 甲、港九方面 › 二、九龍居民禍不單行 (Chinese Wikisource transcription) | The chronicle counted 'more than a thousand' residents inside the City at the deadline of 10 Dec 1947. | "十二月十日限令期滿，九龍城內千餘居民，靜靜地等待命運的宣示" (On 10 Dec the deadline expired; the thousand-odd residents inside Kowloon City quietly waited for their fate.) |
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊 (1 Jan 1948), section 港九澳一年來大事記 (by 唐飄) › 甲、港九方面 › 二、九龍居民禍不單行 (Chinese Wikisource transcription) | The residents held small-group meetings day after day on emergency measures, but stayed calm. | "連日小組集會商討應急辦法，但仍能持鎭定，沒有輕舉妄動的事端發生" (day after day small groups met to discuss emergency measures, but they kept calm and nothing rash happened) |
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊 (1 Jan 1948), section 港九澳一年來大事記 (by 唐飄) › 甲、港九方面 › 二、九龍居民禍不單行 (Chinese Wikisource transcription) | Nanjing reported that the Foreign Ministry made a strong protest to the British Embassy about the order to demolish houses. | "這種抗議係由外交部向英大使館提出，內容重申中國對九龍城主權問題" (This protest was made by the Foreign Ministry to the British Embassy and restated China's sovereignty over Kowloon City.) |
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊 (1 Jan 1948), section 港九澳一年來大事記 (by 唐飄) › 甲、港九方面 › 二、九龍居民禍不單行 (Chinese Wikisource transcription) | At 5 pm on 10 Dec 1947 a foreign Public Works Department officer and three Chinese police sergeants served Kowloon Magistracy summonses on the residents. | "由工務局一外籍職員偕同華警目三人，分持九龍裁判司傳票若干張，向城內各居民送達" (a foreign PWD staff member with three Chinese police sergeants served a number of Kowloon Magistracy summonses on the residents in the City) |
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊 (1 Jan 1948), section 港九澳一年來大事記 (by 唐飄) › 甲、港九方面 › 二、九龍居民禍不單行 (Chinese Wikisource transcription) | The summonses charged the residents with occupying government land on 11 Dec 1947 and told them to appear at 11:30 am on 16 Dec 1947. | "居民於十二月十一日，佔用政府公地，於十二月十六日上午十一時卅分到案候訊" (the residents occupied government land on 11 Dec; to appear for hearing at 11:30 am on 16 Dec) |
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊 (1 Jan 1948), section 港九澳一年來大事記 (by 唐飄) › 甲、港九方面 › 二、九龍居民禍不單行 (Chinese Wikisource transcription) | The residents held a residents' meeting that night and visited the special commissioner on 12 Dec 1947. | "於卽晚召開居民大會謀應付，幷於十二月十二日往謁外交特派員請示" (that night they called a residents' meeting to plan a response, and on 12 Dec went to see the commissioner) |
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊, section 港九澳一年來大事記 › 甲、港九方面 › 一、保障民業論價賠償 (Wikisource) | The district around the City (not the walled City): the Hong Kong government said in May 1947 that the Japanese army had damaged buildings near Kowloon City when it extended Kai Tak airport in 1942. The claimants counted 924 houses demolished by 1 Jun 1947. | "民業損失統計九百萬元，被拆毀房屋共九百二十四棟" (property losses totalled nine million dollars; houses demolished numbered 924) |
| 國民黨駐港澳總支部元旦特刊, directory table 九龍公共汽車 (Wikisource) | The district (not the walled City): in 1947 Kowloon bus route 1 ran from Tsim Sha Tsui to Kowloon City (九龍城) every 5 minutes for 20 cents, and route 11 ran from Jordan Road Pier to Kowloon City every 10 minutes for 20 cents. | "No.11 / 佐頓道碼頭<br />九龍城 / 佐頓道—加士居道—馬頭圍道—譚公道—太子道" (No. 11: Jordan Road Pier to Kowloon City, via Jordan Road, Gascoigne Road, Ma Tau Wai Road, Tam Kung Road, Prince Edward Road) |

- **Leads:**
  - Hong Kong Public Works Department notice of 27 Nov 1947 to the residents of Kowloon City, deadline 10 Dec 1947 (cited).
  - Kowloon Magistracy summonses served 10 Dec 1947 for occupying government land, hearing 16 Dec 1947 (cited).
  - ROC Foreign Ministry protest to the British Embassy, Nanjing, Dec 1947 (cited).
  - Petition of the 「寶安縣九龍城」居民聯合大會 to the Guangdong Provincial Assembly, 6 Dec 1947 (cited).
### top-china-12: 對九龍城事件 時昭瀛發表談話 對事件真象有所說明 外部刻仍與英交涉中 (中央日報 1948-01-10, 第二版; with 社論「抗議九龍城拆屋事件」)

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-20; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-20.
- **Citation:** 中央日報 (Central Daily News, organ of the 國民黨), 10 January 1948, page 2: "對九龍城事件 時昭瀛發表談話 對事件眞象有所說明 外部刻仍與英交涉中" (中央社南京九日電); "外部兩廣特派員 向港督提出要求 釋放被捕華人重建被拆房屋 我方保留此次事件一切法益" (中央社香港九日電); 社論 "抗議九龍城拆屋事件". Internet Archive item zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.10 (collection zhongyang-ribao-archive, uploaded 2021-10-14), https://archive.org/details/zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.10/page/n1
- **Class:** primary (Official statements of the time, reported by the government news agency in the party paper, and an editorial of the same day. It gives the official view of the Republic of China (ROC) of January 1948, not a neutral account. The history of 1898-1899 in the talk is background (kind secondary). Background for 1948, not for the peak period.)
- **Date of the record:** 1948-01-10 (statements of 7-9 Jan 1948)
- **Files:**
  - `top-china-12-n1-c6693c37.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Page image of the Central Daily News 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (leaf n1), with the Foreign Ministry statement, the Special Commissioner item and the editorial on the Kowloon City demolitions; https://archive.org/download/zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.10/page/n1.jpg; 7762231 bytes; SHA-256 `629ae6f7548523061f8fb59b2f28abbae39cfa5344923cd93a45447f646e7569`; licence: No licence statement; the archive.org item has no rights or licenseurl field; the uploader gives no rights (https://archive.org/details/zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.10)
- **Note:** its 3 secondary facts (narration, later captions) are in the secondary twin, [`../../secondary-sources/china-official-records-1933-1987/SOURCE.md`](../../secondary-sources/china-official-records-1933-1987/SOURCE.md).

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "對九龍城事件 時昭瀛發表談話 對事件眞象有所說明 外部刻仍與英交涉中" (中央社 Nanjing 9 Jan), opening | On 9 Jan 1948, in Nanjing, 時昭瀛, Director of the Information Department of the Foreign Ministry (外部情報司長), gave a statement on the Kowloon City affair. | "外部情報司長時昭瀛，九日就九龍城事件發表談話" (時昭瀛, Director of the Information Department of the Foreign Ministry, on the 9th made a statement on the Kowloon City affair) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "對九龍城事件 時昭瀛發表談話 對事件眞象有所說明 外部刻仍與英交涉中" (中央社 Nanjing 9 Jan), upper tier | The statement described the City as an earthen walled town (土城) of some tens of Chinese mu inside the leased territory, whose walls had long been in ruins. | "面積約數十華畝之土城，其城牆已年久傾毀" (an earthen walled town of an area of some tens of Chinese mu, whose walls have long since collapsed) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "對九龍城事件 時昭瀛發表談話 對事件眞象有所說明 外部刻仍與英交涉中" (中央社 Nanjing 9 Jan), upper tier, left | The Ministry gave the Hong Kong reasons for the removal. First: all the huts had been built after the war; the old landowners had taken compensation before the war; the hut builders had no title to the land and no permission. | "各該木屋均係此次戰後所建，原有地主已於戰前領取補償金" (all these wooden huts were built after this war; the original landowners had already received compensation before the war) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "對九龍城事件 時昭瀛發表談話 對事件眞象有所說明 外部刻仍與英交涉中" (中央社 Nanjing 9 Jan), upper tier, left | Second: Hong Kong said that it pulled the huts down only for fire prevention and the prevention of epidemics, for the safety of the public. | "純係根據防火防疫，爲一般市民謀安全之理由" (purely on the grounds of fire prevention and epidemic prevention, for the safety of the general public) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "對九龍城事件 時昭瀛發表談話 對事件眞象有所說明 外部刻仍與英交涉中" (中央社 Nanjing 9 Jan), upper and lower tier | The Ministry said that it had asked Britain not to act alone before the question of jurisdiction was settled, and not to leave the poor residents of the City homeless. | "勿使九龍城內貧苦居民，無家可歸" (do not make the poor residents inside Kowloon City homeless) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "對九龍城事件 時昭瀛發表談話 對事件眞象有所說明 外部刻仍與英交涉中" (中央社 Nanjing 9 Jan), lower tier | The Hong Kong Government extended the removal deadline more than once and at first used no force. | "港政府於原定遷移限期屆滿時，曾一再延期" (when the original removal deadline expired, the Hong Kong Government extended it again and again) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "對九龍城事件 時昭瀛發表談話 對事件眞象有所說明 外部刻仍與英交涉中" (中央社 Nanjing 9 Jan), lower tier | On 5 Jan 1948 the Hong Kong Government sent police and labourers into the City, pulled down the wooden huts by force, and arrested two residents' representatives. | "港政府忽派警員夫役等，進入九龍城，強制拆遷該地木屋，並逮捕當地居民代表二人" (the Hong Kong Government suddenly sent police and labourers into Kowloon City, forcibly demolished the wooden huts there, and arrested two local residents' representatives) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "對九龍城事件 時昭瀛發表談話 對事件眞象有所說明 外部刻仍與英交涉中" (中央社 Nanjing 9 Jan), lower tier | The Ministry made an oral protest to the British Embassy on 6 Jan 1948 and a formal protest on 8 Jan. | "本部即於一月六日向英大使館提口頭抗議" (the Ministry on 6 January made an oral protest to the British Embassy) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "對九龍城事件 時昭瀛發表談話 對事件眞象有所說明 外部刻仍與英交涉中" (中央社 Nanjing 9 Jan), bottom tier | The Ministry asked Britain to stop the demolitions at once, to release the arrested residents, to make no more arrests, and to resettle the residents who had suffered. | "立即停止此強制拆遷措施，釋放被捕居民，不再繼續逮捕" (stop these forcible demolition measures at once, release the arrested residents, make no further arrests) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "對九龍城事件 時昭瀛發表談話 對事件眞象有所說明 外部刻仍與英交涉中" (中央社 Nanjing 9 Jan), bottom tier | On 7 Jan 1948 the Ministry's Special Commissioner for Guangdong and Guangxi (駐兩廣特派員) protested to the Governor of Hong Kong, asked for the release of the two arrested residents, and tried to get relief for the evicted residents. | "及設法救濟被迫遷之居民" (and to find ways to relieve the residents who were forced to move) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "外部兩廣特派員 向港督提出要求 釋放被捕華人重建被拆房屋 我方保留此次事件一切法益" (中央社 Hong Kong 9 Jan), para. 1 | The Special Commissioner was 郭德華. On 7 Jan he met the Governor and made China's fifth protest. | "外部特派員郭德華，七日晤港督作第五次抗議" (the Foreign Ministry Special Commissioner 郭德華 met the Governor on the 7th and made a fifth protest) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "外部兩廣特派員 向港督提出要求 釋放被捕華人重建被拆房屋 我方保留此次事件一切法益" (中央社 Hong Kong 9 Jan), demands (2)-(4) | 郭 demanded that the arrested residents 朱沛唐 and 劉毅夫 be freed at once, that the residents' demolished houses be rebuilt at once, and that Britain bear all the consequences. China reserved all its rights in the affair. | "原住居民被拆房屋，須即予重建" (the demolished houses of the original residents must be rebuilt at once) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "外部兩廣特派員 向港督提出要求 釋放被捕華人重建被拆房屋 我方保留此次事件一切法益" (中央社 Hong Kong 9 Jan), para. 2 | INFERRED: Before 5 Jan, 郭 had met the Governor four times on the demolitions: on 29 Nov, on 2 Dec (the day is worn), on 18 Dec and on 23 Dec 1947. | "十一月二十九日、十二月二日、十二月十八日、十二月二十三等四日四次晤港督" (met the Governor four times, on four days: 29 November, 2 December, 18 December and 23 December) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), editorial (社論) "抗議九龍城拆屋事件", opening | The editorial says, after a 中央社 report, that the Hong Kong Government pulled down 74 houses (椽) in the City on the morning of 5 Jan 1948. | "香港政府於五日晨強迫拆毀九龍城民房七十四椽" (on the morning of the 5th the Hong Kong Government forcibly demolished seventy-four houses in Kowloon City) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 10 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), editorial (社論) "抗議九龍城拆屋事件", opening | The editorial gives the loss of houses and property as HK$244,800, after a 中央社 report: more than 4,000 million yuan (四十餘億元) at the official rate. It says this is only the loss of what was destroyed. The residents were scattered and had to find other shelter, could not work well, some had no food, and illness and death followed; the editorial says these losses are not counted. | "房屋及財產損失爲港幣二十四萬四千八百元" (the loss of houses and property was HK$244,800) |

- **Leads:**
  - Chinese Foreign Ministry protest notes to the British Embassy of 6 Jan (oral) and 8 Jan 1948 (written) on the Kowloon City demolitions.
  - Record of the meetings of Special Commissioner 郭德華 with the Governor of Hong Kong on 29 Nov, 2 Dec, 18 Dec, 23 Dec 1947 and 7 Jan 1948 (in the Foreign Ministry files of 國史館 or 近史所, and in the Colonial Office files).
  - 中央社 report of about 6-9 Jan 1948 that gives 74 houses demolished and a loss of HK$244,800 (cited by the editorial).
### top-china-13: 九龍城昨發生不幸事件 我居民六人受槍傷 港警竟使用催淚彈强拆陋屋 王外長召見英使令中止行動 (中央日報 1948-01-13, 第二版)

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-20; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-20.
- **Citation:** 中央日報 (Central Daily News), 13 January 1948, page 2: "九龍城昨發生不幸事件 我居民六人受槍傷 港警竟使用催淚彈强拆陋屋 王外長召見英使令中止行動" (中央社香港十二日急電 and other 中央社 dispatches of 12 Jan); boxed "政府令宋子文救濟九龍居民" (中央社南京十二日電); "外部致英政府抗議 要求港府停止行動" (中央社南京十二日電). Internet Archive item zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.13, https://archive.org/details/zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.13/page/n1
- **Class:** primary (News dispatches of the government news agency on the day of the events, and the text of an official protest note. The ROC view, not neutral. Background for 1948, not for the peak period.)
- **Date of the record:** 1948-01-13 (events of 12 Jan 1948; protest note of 8 Jan 1948)
- **Files:**
  - `top-china-13-n1-7bd82b29.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Page image of the Central Daily News 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (leaf n1), with the reports of the shooting in the City on 12 Jan 1948 and the Foreign Ministry protest note; https://archive.org/download/zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.13/page/n1.jpg; 7366922 bytes; SHA-256 `c64b85be8bae5da73f645ca7bc86fdc025bb6f27219e0f9f0ba310b17c8d5b9d`; licence: No licence statement; the archive.org item has no rights or licenseurl field; the uploader gives no rights (https://archive.org/details/zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.13)

The clash lasted about half an hour. | "我國居民六人爲步槍彈所傷，港警並投催淚彈，致另有其他居民八人負傷" (six of our residents were wounded by rifle bullets; the police also threw tear gas, so eight other residents were hurt) |

The police opened fire and threw tear gas. | "乃出而抗拒，港警遂開槍射擊，並投催淚彈" (so they came out to resist; the Hong Kong police then opened fire and threw tear gas) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "九龍城昨發生不幸事件 我居民六人受槍傷 港警竟使用催淚彈强拆陋屋 王外長召見英使令中止行動" (中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, urgent), 中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, para. 2 | 張忠武, a representative of the residents' association (九龍居民協會), was the first to be hit, by a bullet in the head. Two of the five residents in this count were women. All were taken to Kowloon Hospital (九龍醫院). | "首先被槍彈擊中頭部負傷" (was the first to be hit in the head by a bullet) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "九龍城昨發生不幸事件 我居民六人受槍傷 港警竟使用催淚彈强拆陋屋 王外長召見英使令中止行動" (中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, urgent), 中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, para. 3 | The office of the Foreign Ministry Special Commissioner said that the Hong Kong authorities had given no notice before they pulled down the temporary huts that the residents had rebuilt on the ruins. | "並未於昨日提出通知" (and gave no notice yesterday) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "九龍城昨發生不幸事件 我居民六人受槍傷 港警竟使用催淚彈强拆陋屋 王外長召見英使令中止行動" (中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, urgent), 中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, para. 3 | The rebuilt huts were makeshift: most had walls on three sides, made of boards nailed up, with a roof of galvanised sheet (鉛皮). The evicted poor had slept in the open before they built them. | "大率三面是牆，釘以木板，並以鉛皮蓋頂而成" (for the most part three sides are walls, nailed up with boards, and they are roofed with lead [galvanised] sheet) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "九龍城昨發生不幸事件 我居民六人受槍傷 港警竟使用催淚彈强拆陋屋 王外長召見英使令中止行動" (中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, urgent), 中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, para. 3 | At the time of the dispatch a large body of Hong Kong police still stood in the old City, to stop the poor from rebuilding their homes, with tear gas ready. | "仍有大批港警駐於九龍舊城區" (a large body of Hong Kong police was still stationed in the old City area) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "九龍城昨發生不幸事件 我居民六人受槍傷 港警竟使用催淚彈强拆陋屋 王外長召見英使令中止行動" (中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, urgent), list of the wounded | INFERRED: The names of the wounded: seriously wounded 梁創 and 王賴鳳英; lightly wounded 張忠武, 張炳林, 劉三妹, 孫日 and others. The second character of 王賴鳳英 is read from worn print. | "受重傷者梁創、王賴鳳英、受輕傷者張忠武、張炳林、劉三妹、孫日等" (seriously wounded: 梁創, 王賴鳳英; lightly wounded: 張忠武, 張炳林, 劉三妹, 孫日 and others) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "九龍城昨發生不幸事件 我居民六人受槍傷 港警竟使用催淚彈强拆陋屋 王外長召見英使令中止行動" (中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, urgent), 中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, second and third items | An official count gave six residents of the City wounded. The Hong Kong Government said five people were in treatment, two seriously and four lightly hurt; the numbers in the paper do not agree. | "港政府聲稱五人刻在治療中" (the Hong Kong Government states that five people are now under treatment) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "九龍城昨發生不幸事件 我居民六人受槍傷 港警竟使用催淚彈强拆陋屋 王外長召見英使令中止行動" (中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, urgent), 中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, third item | An eyewitness from the area said that at least six were hurt, and that a child was hurt whose name was not on the list. The hospital doctors, nurses and staff told reporters nothing about the number of the wounded. | "並有小孩一人其名姓未列入死傷名單中" (and there was a child whose name was not entered in the list of the dead and wounded) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), boxed item "政府令宋子文救濟九龍居民" | On the afternoon of 12 Jan the Government in Nanjing ordered 宋子文, chairman of Guangdong Province, to give the necessary relief to the City residents. | "立即電令粵省主席宋子文作必要之救濟" (at once telegraphed the chairman of Guangdong Province, 宋子文, to give the necessary relief) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "九龍城昨發生不幸事件 我居民六人受槍傷 港警竟使用催淚彈强拆陋屋 王外長召見英使令中止行動" (中央社 Hong Kong 12 Jan, urgent), 中央社 Nanjing 12 Jan | Foreign Minister 王 called in the British Ambassador. He asked that London tell the Governor of Hong Kong to stop all coercive action in the City while the two governments negotiated, and to send no more police in. | "嚴厲要求倫敦政府立即訓令香港總督" (sternly demanded that the London government at once instruct the Governor of Hong Kong) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "外部致英政府抗議 要求港府停止行動 根據中英展拓香港界址專條 九龍城之管轄權應屬於我國" (中央社 Nanjing 12 Jan), text of the note | The Foreign Ministry's written protest of 8 Jan 1948 says that the Hong Kong Government ordered the Chinese residents of the City to pull down their houses and move on 27 Nov 1947. | "於一九四七年十一月二十七日飭令九龍城內中國居民拆遷房屋以來" (since on 27 November 1947 [it] ordered the Chinese residents inside Kowloon City to pull down their houses and move) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "外部致英政府抗議 要求港府停止行動 根據中英展拓香港界址專條 九龍城之管轄權應屬於我國" (中央社 Nanjing 12 Jan), text of the note | The note says that on 10 Dec 1947 the Minister himself asked the British Ambassador to China for a friendly settlement. | "外交部長親向英國駐華大使表示" (the Foreign Minister personally told the British Ambassador to China) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "外部致英政府抗議 要求港府停止行動 根據中英展拓香港界址專條 九龍城之管轄權應屬於我國" (中央社 Nanjing 12 Jan), text of the note | INFERRED: The note says that on 5 Jan 1948 armed police and labourers went into the City, pulled down some tens of residents' houses, and arrested two residents' representatives. The number of houses is read from a worn column. | "強拆九龍城內居民房屋數十間，並逮捕當地居民代表二人" (forcibly pulled down some tens of houses of residents inside Kowloon City, and arrested two local residents' representatives) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 13 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "外部致英政府抗議 要求港府停止行動 根據中英展拓香港界址專條 九龍城之管轄權應屬於我國" (中央社 Nanjing 12 Jan), text of the note, lower tier | INFERRED: The note says that the Hong Kong Government gave as its reason for the removal that the land was "British Crown land" (英國皇家官地). China says that the jurisdiction of the City was never given up. The reading of this part is from a creased column. | "係屬「英國皇家官地」" (that it is "British Crown land") |

- **Leads:**
  - Chinese Foreign Ministry written protest note of 8 Jan 1948 to the British Embassy (full text in the ROC Foreign Ministry files, 國史館 or 近史所).
  - Hong Kong Government order of 27 Nov 1947 to the Chinese residents of the City to pull down their houses and move.
  - Kowloon Hospital (九龍醫院) records of the wounded of 12 Jan 1948 (梁創, 王賴鳳英, 張忠武 and others).
### top-china-14: 社論：由九龍城事件測驗英帝國主義 (中央日報 1948-01-14, 第二版; with 全國各地同聲憤慨 抗議港府侵權暴行 京滬學生醞釀示威遊行)

- **Status:** Partly read on 2026-11-20; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-20. I read page 2 (BookReader leaf n1) at half scale (3052x4166 px) with enlarged crops. I read all of the news item on the protests (dispatches from Canton, Nanjing and Shanghai of 13 Jan) and the opening of the editorial. I skimmed the rest of the long editorial: it argues about British imperialism and the treaty of 1898, with no new facts on the City or its people, so I did not transcribe it. The OCR text is garbled; I read the images. The page gives the national reaction, not life in the City.
- **Citation:** 中央日報 (Central Daily News), 14 January 1948, page 2: 社論 "由九龍城事件測驗英帝國主義"; "全國各地同聲憤慨 抗議港府侵權暴行 京滬學生醞釀示威遊行" (本報廣州十三日專電, 本報南京十三日專電, 本報上海十三日專電, 中央社廣州十三日電). Internet Archive item zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.14 (uploaded 2021-10-14), https://archive.org/details/zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.14/page/n1
- **Class:** primary (An editorial and news dispatches of the time in the party paper of the Kuomintang (國民黨). It is the official ROC line and a record of the reaction in China, not a neutral account. Background for 1948, not for the peak period.)
- **Date of the record:** 1948-01-14 (events of 12-13 Jan 1948)
- **Files:**
  - `top-china-14-n1-602d2ecd.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Page image of the Central Daily News 14 Jan 1948, page 2 (leaf n1), with the editorial on the Kowloon City affair and the report of protests in China; https://archive.org/download/zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.14/page/n1.jpg; 7598927 bytes; SHA-256 `81ccb5883f5681a7e3915e8e628717190229e396a68a51f41013f8477fa87999`; licence: No licence statement; the archive.org item has no rights or licenseurl field; the uploader gives no rights (https://archive.org/details/zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.14)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 14 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), editorial (社論) "由九龍城事件測驗英帝國主義", para. 1 | The editorial says that the paper had protested before against the forced demolition of the City residents' houses, and that the paper did not care about "those few tens of broken houses" but about China's self-respect. | "我們並不重視那幾十椽破屋，實在爲了我們的自尊心" (we do not care about those few tens of broken houses; it is really for our self-respect) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 14 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), editorial (社論) "由九龍城事件測驗英帝國主義", para. 1 | The editorial repeats that six people who lived in temporary sheds were shot and eight were hurt by tear gas in the City on 12 Jan 1948. | "被槍傷的六人，被催淚彈傷害的八人" (six wounded by gunshot, eight hurt by tear gas) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 14 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), editorial (社論) "由九龍城事件測驗英帝國主義", para. 1 | The editorial says that the Government had ordered the Guangdong provincial government to give relief to the City residents, and that the Foreign Ministry had protested to Ambassador 施諦文. | "政府除令粵省府對九龍城居民作必要救濟外" (the Government, besides ordering the Guangdong provincial government to give the City residents the necessary relief) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 14 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "全國各地同聲憤慨 抗議港府侵權暴行 京滬學生醞釀示威遊行", 本報廣州十三日專電 | In Canton on 13 Jan 1948 the citizens planned a demonstration about the Kowloon affair. In Bao'an county (寶安) a mass meeting passed resolutions, among them to stop food supplies to Hong Kong. This is after a United Press report. | "停運食物供給香港" (stop sending food supplies to Hong Kong) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 14 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "全國各地同聲憤慨 抗議港府侵權暴行 京滬學生醞釀示威遊行", 本報廣州十三日專電 | A demand of the protests: Hong Kong must pay for the losses of the residents, free the two arrested men, and promise that no such event would happen again. | "賠償被難居民損失" (compensate the losses of the residents who suffered) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 14 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "全國各地同聲憤慨 抗議港府侵權暴行 京滬學生醞釀示威遊行", 本報南京十三日專電 | In Nanjing, students of the universities and middle schools prepared a demonstration; Central University (中央大學), Chengchi University (政治大學) and others exchanged views. | "學生醞釀之遊行又再集議" (the students again met to plan the march) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 14 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "全國各地同聲憤慨 抗議港府侵權暴行 京滬學生醞釀示威遊行", 本報上海十三日專電 | In Shanghai on 12 Jan, a meeting of the department representatives of Chiao Tung University (交通大學) resolved to issue a declaration against the Kowloon affair, to send comfort to the City residents, and to move the union of universities and middle schools to denounce Britain. The other students of the city said they agreed. | "慰問九龍居民" (to comfort the Kowloon residents) |
| Central Daily News (中央日報) 14 Jan 1948, page 2 (第二版; scan leaf n1), article "全國各地同聲憤慨 抗議港府侵權暴行 京滬學生醞釀示威遊行", 中央社廣州十三日電 | A 中央社 dispatch from Canton of 13 Jan repeated that the Hong Kong police went into the City again on 12 Jan and shot and wounded six residents, and that all circles in Canton were angry. | "香港警察十二日再度進入九龍城，槍傷居民六人" (the Hong Kong police on the 12th again entered Kowloon City and shot and wounded six residents) |
### top-china-19: 王世杰施諦文商獲臨時方案解决九龍砦城事件 (華僑日報 1948-01-17)

| NPWK19480117 leaf n0 (front page), article 王世杰施諦文商獲臨時方案解决九龍砦城事件, 合衆社上海十六日電 | A United Press dispatch says that the Ambassador agreed to refer the Chinese demands to London, and that the talks centred on the status of Kowloon City. | "雙方所討論焦點，集中于九龍城之中英地位問題" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n0 (front page), article 王世杰施諦文商獲臨時方案解决九龍砦城事件, 美聯社南京十六日電 | An Associated Press dispatch quotes a British Embassy spokesman: London will not give way on the question of jurisdiction over Kowloon City. | "倫敦對於九龍城治權問題，將不致有所讓步" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n0 (front page), article 王世杰施諦文商獲臨時方案解决九龍砦城事件 | The Ambassador and the Foreign Minister reached a "temporary plan" in a long meeting on 16 Jan 1948, but the Ambassador did not give its details. | "曾獲致一項「臨時方案」以解決九龍城問題" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n0 (front page), article 王世杰施諦文商獲臨時方案解决九龍砦城事件 | Twenty members of the Control Yuan (監察院) asked the government to take steps at once to recover Hong Kong and Kowloon. | "監察院有廿名委員" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n0 (front page), article 王世杰施諦文商獲臨時方案解决九龍砦城事件, 【本報特訊】南京息 | The director of the European Department of the Foreign Ministry, 尹葆宇, asked a counsellor of the British Embassy that Hong Kong release the arrested residents' representatives and not force the residents to move. | "不得迫令當地居民他遷" |

On 16 Jan 1948 he asked the residents association for its register of households, people and losses, to plan the share-out of relief money. The given name of 鄺 is not clear in the scan. | "特派員通知居民會，將登記冊及有關資料呈閱" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | INFERRED: The commissioner asked to see the representatives 曾生 and 陳振洲. They were busy, thus 羅叔烈 and 劉子良 crossed the harbour at 8 pm to see him. I read the surname 羅 in 羅叔烈; an earlier read of 10 Dec 1947 (top-china-8) gives 劉叔烈. | "故由羅叔烈與劉子良二人於夜八時渡海晉謁" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | The reporter had the household, population and loss figures of the whole City from the register, but the association asked the paper not to print them until it corrected them. Thus the figures are not in the paper. | "謂該登記冊數字仍欠詳晰，請於更正後始行發表" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | The commissioner and his staff visited the injured residents in Kowloon Hospital (九龍醫院). | "往九龍醫院慰問傷者" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n3 (local news page), same article, lower part | INFERRED: The commissioner then met leaders of Hong Kong and Kowloon associations. He said that his two tasks were to comfort the people of Kowloon City and to help with relief. The relief work was to be run with a Bao'an county magistrate surnamed 王 (the 20 Jan issue confirms 寶安王縣長). | "奉宋主席命來港慰問" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n3 (local news page), article 晚上張設帆幕十個 龍城居民集團住宿 | On 16 Jan 1948 the residents association bought ten canvas tents, because the evicted residents were scattered and hard to gather. Four large tents held 100 people each, two medium tents 50 each, and four small tents 20 each. They were put up on wooden poles at night as group sleeping places. | "大者四個，每個可容百人，中者兩個，每個可容五十人，細者四個，每個可容二十人" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | Donated cotton blankets and cotton vests went to people with no bedding: one blanket for each household, and one vest for each adult and child pair. | "棉毡每戶一張，棉背心則每一成人與一小孩共佔一件" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n3 (local news page), article 張忠武今日可出院 居民會公布收支 | INFERRED: Two residents injured in the demolition, 薛日 (the second character is not clear) and 劉三妹, were well again and left hospital on 16 Jan 1948. The others improved, except 梁創. 張忠武 was expected to leave hospital on 17 Jan. | "其餘傷者，除梁創外，均有進步" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | The residents association paid the injured from its relief money: 30 dollars to each seriously injured person and 15 dollars to each lightly injured person. | "重傷者每人三十元，輕傷者每人十五元" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | On 16 Jan 1948 the association gave each resident one catty of rice, half a pound of biscuits and 8 dollars in cash. | "發米每人一斤，餅干半磅，現款八元" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | INFERRED: The association published its accounts: from 8 to 15 Jan 1948 it received 10,973,500 yuan in national currency and about 7,700 Hong Kong dollars. The last digits of the Hong Kong dollar sum are not clear in the scan. | "共收國幣一千零九十七萬三千五百元" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n3 (local news page), same article, donor list | INFERRED: The donors included a school that gave four sacks of rice and cash (華南中學), a restaurant trade group that gave 400 cotton vests, and a group in 石硤尾 that gave 200 catties of rice and 11 pounds of biscuits. I read these names from a worn list. | "米四草包" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n3 (local news page), article 朱劉案 决不上訴 (昨日晚九龍城訊) | The representative 陳振洲 said that 朱沛唐 and 劉毅夫 had said they would appeal, but the association decided not to appeal. It held that the arrest of the two men was not a matter for a Hong Kong court and waited for the Chinese diplomats to win their release. | "故決定不進行上訴" |
| NPWK19480117 leaf n3 (local news page), box 讀者振濟龍城同胞 | The paper ran its own readers' relief list for the people of the City; one donor was the machine department staff of the Hongkong Hotel (香港大酒店), 40 dollars. | "香港大酒店機器部全人肆拾元" |

- **Leads:**
  - Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) of about 15-16 Jan 1948: the first report of the visit of the provincial commissioner 鄺 ("詳情經誌前報")
  - The register (登記冊) of households, people and losses of the City residents association, Jan 1948: the paper did not print the figures
### top-china-20: 九龍城事件及沙面事件循外交途徑解决施諦文訪王世杰在外部舉行談判 (華僑日報 1948-01-20)

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-20; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-20.
- **Citation:** Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報), Hong Kong, 20 Jan 1948, front page (leaf n0) and local news (leaf n3), item NPWK19480120, https://archive.org/details/NPWK19480120; and 21 Jan 1948, leaves n0 and n3, item NPWK19480121, https://archive.org/details/NPWK19480121; scans on the Internet Archive, read 2026-11-20.
- **Class:** primary (A Hong Kong daily newspaper of the day, with reports on 19-20 Jan 1948. The tree primary is correct.)
- **Date of the record:** 20 and 21 Jan 1948 (reports of 19-20 Jan 1948)
- **Files:**
  - `top-china-20-n0-deeb5a69.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Page image of Wah Kiu Yat Po 20 Jan 1948, front page, with the talks and the Canton City Council article; https://archive.org/download/NPWK19480120/page/n0.jpg; 5188149 bytes; SHA-256 `cb27f48d1476f724ab1046f1ab1f365b9b66d735a679cc8d7cd52ed6e9729230`; licence: No licence statement; the archive.org item has no rights or licenseurl field; copyright of the newspaper is not stated (https://archive.org/details/NPWK19480120)
  - `top-china-20-n3-a46fc697.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Page image of Wah Kiu Yat Po 20 Jan 1948, local news, with the calm day at the City (19 Jan) and the residents' meeting; https://archive.org/download/NPWK19480120/page/n3.jpg; 5361097 bytes; SHA-256 `3c572d926d0732dd2c84e197ae116047aeb475df70edbcef0adfde1cc6f5a8cb`; licence: No licence statement; the archive.org item has no rights or licenseurl field; copyright of the newspaper is not stated (https://archive.org/details/NPWK19480120)
  - `top-china-20-n0-fb5cbeed.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Page image of Wah Kiu Yat Po 21 Jan 1948, front page, with the talks and the end of the removal order; https://archive.org/download/NPWK19480121/page/n0.jpg; 5216257 bytes; SHA-256 `bbbebd3506cb3bc01e49d7730ec5d6628724f8103e162261851c0592042db0c5`; licence: No licence statement; the archive.org item has no rights or licenseurl field; copyright of the newspaper is not stated (https://archive.org/details/NPWK19480121)
  - `top-china-20-n3-87f14ae4.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Page image of Wah Kiu Yat Po 21 Jan 1948, local news, with the tents of surplus military canvas and the government denial; https://archive.org/download/NPWK19480121/page/n3.jpg; 5224691 bytes; SHA-256 `3e1028a9026a78955414797d5cab9c9ef6d499c8a07cbfff435d794d97d38674`; licence: No licence statement; the archive.org item has no rights or licenseurl field; copyright of the newspaper is not stated (https://archive.org/details/NPWK19480121)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPWK19480120 leaf n0 (front page), lead 九龍城事件及沙面事件循外交途徑解决 | On 19 Jan 1948 the government awarded the Foreign Ministry's commissioner for Guangdong and Guangxi, 郭德華, the Order of the Brilliant Star, third class (三等景星勳章). He was then in Hong Kong for the talks on Kowloon. | "十九日由國府授三等景星勳章" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n0 (front page), 美聯社南京十九日電 | An Associated Press dispatch reports a claim that the Shameen riot aimed to make Britain stop Chinese opposition groups in Hong Kong, in exchange for China not pressing on jurisdiction over Kowloon. The Foreign Ministry information director 時昭瀛 said that this was baseless. | "此種解釋，全無根據" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n0 (front page), article 穗市參議會要求派兵入九龍城護僑 | The Canton City Council opened its fourth session on 19 Jan 1948. A councillor said that 朱克勤 and others would move that the government send troops into Kowloon City to protect the Chinese there, and remove 王世杰 and 郭德華 from office. | "由政府派兵開入九龍城，武裝護僑" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n0 (front page), 合衆社上海十九日電 | INFERRED: A United Press dispatch from Shanghai repeats a Canton rumour that Hong Kong had warned the residents to leave, and that Canton seamen's and dock workers would boycott Hong Kong within 30 minutes of a forced removal. The Hong Kong government denied the warning on 20 Jan (see the 21 Jan items). | "即宣告「杯葛香港」" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n3 (local news page), article 昨日九龍城平安渡過 莫警司否認有行動 | A rumour had said that the police would force out the evicted hut dwellers on the morning of 19 Jan 1948. A reporter reached the City at about 6 am and saw no demolition. | "記者於昨晨六時許抵達九龍寨城後" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | INFERRED: On the cleared hut site the reporter saw four green canvas tents near the East Gate, and the national flag of the Republic of China flying. I read the second landmark as a paper-burning pavilion (字紙亭); the characters are worn. | "草綠色之帆布帳幕四幅" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | Women and children had all been sent elsewhere. The site of the huts held only broken tiles and stones. | "婦孺皆已全部疏散他處" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | After 8 am the officers of the residents association came back to their office, a space fenced with bamboo and wood. | "以竹木圍起之辦事處" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | INFERRED: Police were still posted on the hill nearby. A police superintendent surnamed 莫 told the paper that the police had made no decision to remove anyone; at the demolitions they had only kept order for the Public Works Department. | "僅為協助工務局執行職務時維持秩序而已" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n3 (local news page), same article, last part | The residents association met on the night of 18 Jan 1948 and gave each person a task for an emergency. Each resident got 7 dollars, and each person aged 16 or over could get a cotton vest. | "十六歲以上者可領棉背心一件" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n3 (local news page), same article, last part | INFERRED: Only 20 to 30 able-bodied men stayed on guard at the site; ten of them sat up all night. The last words are worn. | "僅留壯丁二三十人看守" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n3 (local news page), article 九龍城居民昨夜召開大會 | A delegate of the residents association was still in Canton. He wrote home to tell the residents to keep working; 劉子良 quoted two phrases of the letter to the reporter. | "開花結果，心堅石穿" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | INFERRED: On 19 Jan 1948 the residents association sent two men with the Bao'an county magistrate surnamed 王 to take the 320 million yuan of relief money left from the provincial gift to the 順興 rice shop. They were to buy 2?,089 catties 12 taels of rice (二萬?千零八十九斤十二兩), thus more than 20,000 catties; the thousands digit is not clear. The shop did not have enough rice, thus payment and delivery were set for 23 Jan. | "救濟金三億二千萬元，往順興米店購買白米" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | The residents association held a general meeting of the residents at 9 pm on 19 Jan 1948. | "居民會昨夜九時召開居民大會" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n3 (local news page), item 據港府發言人稱 | A government spokesman said that of the City residents taken to hospital with injuries on 12 Jan 1948, three had left hospital, two more would leave soon, and one was out of danger. | "有三人經已痊愈出院" |
| NPWK19480120 leaf n3 (local news page), item 昨天氣稍覺寒冷 | On 19 Jan 1948 the weather was cold. Kowloon City had much open ground and blowing dust. Many visitors still came to comfort the evicted residents, among them children. | "曠地甚多，沙塵滾滾" |
| NPWK19480121 leaf n0 (front page), lead 九龍城事件談判好轉 | 19 Jan 1948 was the last day of the Hong Kong government's order for the residents to move, and the government did not enforce it. | "十九日為香港政府下令九龍居民拆遷之最後一日，港府亦未強制執行" |
| NPWK19480121 leaf n0 (front page), same lead | On 20 Jan 1948 the Foreign Ministry said the talks on Kowloon City were going better. The British Ambassador made a written claim for the loss of the British consulate in Canton. | "九龍城事件漸趨好轉" |
| NPWK19480121 leaf n0 (front page), 華聯社南京廿日電 | The Foreign Ministry said that a report of a new protest on the treatment of the City residents was a translation error: it had only said the talks went on. | "由于翻譯上之錯誤" |
| NPWK19480121 leaf n3 (local news page), article 港當局正式否認限龍城居民遷出 | On 20 Jan 1948 the evicted residents waited for the diplomats. The main work of the residents association was to give out relief goods. | "至該寨城之居民聯合會之大部工作，仍為將所收救濟品發給" |
| NPWK19480121 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | INFERRED: Green canvas tents on the old hut site were the temporary home of residents who had found no other place. The tents were surplus military stores and kept out wind and rain. The number of tents is not clear in the scan. | "此帳幕原為剩餘軍品，可蔽風雨" |
| NPWK19480121 leaf n3 (local news page), same article | The national flag flew over the rubble, and police still patrolled nearby from time to time. | "惟仍有警察不時在附近巡邏" |
| NPWK19480121 leaf n3 (local news page), item 【特訊】政府新聞處稱 | The Government Information Services denied a rumour that the police had told the people in Kowloon City to leave by a set time. | "此等謠言，實非事實" |
| NPWK19480121 leaf n3 (local news page), donor list | INFERRED: A list of gifts for the City residents on 19 Jan 1948 includes the women of a church in Kowloon City (三一堂婦女), 10 dollars. The first character after 九龍城 is worn. | "三一堂婦女十元" |

- **Leads:**
  - 華僑日報 21 Jan 1948: the item 澳門車夫義拉賑濟龍城同胞 (in the issue headline list; I did not find it on leaves n0 or n3)
  - The motions on Kowloon City at the fourth session of the Canton City Council (廣州市參議會), opened 19 Jan 1948
### china-2: 外交部严重抗议英国政府侵犯我国主权

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-18; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-20.
- **Citation:** Xinhua News Agency (新華社), 17 Jan 1963: 「香港英国当局企图强拆九龙城寨房屋迫迁居民 外交部严重抗议英国政府侵犯我国主权」, People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, p. 1. Read on the 老資料網 mirror: https://www.laoziliao.net/rmrb/1963-01-18-1#301938
- **Class:** primary (A Xinhua news report of the PRC protest, printed the next day. Its account of 1960-1963 is of recent events; only the claim of historic sovereignty is background.)
- **Date of the record:** 1963-01-17 (Xinhua dispatch); printed 1963-01-18

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | Xinhua gave the City's location as the north-east of Kowloon, and its population as more than 20,000. | "九龙城寨位于九龙东北部，人口有两万余人" (Kowloon Walled City is in north-east Kowloon, with a population of more than 20,000.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | Xinhua said that in recent years the Hong Kong authorities had tried many times to demolish City houses and move the residents, and that the residents' opposition had stopped them each time. | "近几年来，香港英国当局多次企图强拆城寨房屋，迁移当地居民，但均遭受居民的反对而未能得逞" (In recent years the British Hong Kong authorities attempted many times to demolish houses in the City and move residents, but failed because of the residents' opposition.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | In May 1960 the Hong Kong authorities published the 'North-east Kowloon outline development plan', which put the City inside the plan area. Residents protested to the Town Planning Board. | "一九六○年五月，香港英国当局发表了“九龙东北部发展草图计划”，竟把九龙城寨列入其计划范围之内" (In May 1960 the British Hong Kong authorities published the 'North-east Kowloon outline development plan' and included Kowloon Walled City in its scope.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | On 19 Mar 1962 the Resettlement Department sent more than 30 people into the City. They put up resettlement notices and registered residents on Tung Tau Tsuen Road (東頭村道), Lung Shing Road (龍城路), Lung Chun Road (龍津路) and Kwong Ming Street (光明街). | "一九六二年三月十九日，香港英国徙置事务处派出三十多人，进入九龙城寨的东头村道、龙城路、龙津路、光明街等街道上" (On 19 March 1962 the Resettlement Department sent more than thirty people into the streets of the City: Tung Tau Tsuen Road, Lung Shing Road, Lung Chun Road, Kwong Ming Street.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | The Resettlement Department said that the houses on these streets would be demolished under the 'Tung Tau Tsuen Phase 2 resettlement scheme'. | "根据“东头村第二期徙置计划”，要把上述街道的房屋拆除" (under the 'Tung Tau Tsuen Phase 2 resettlement scheme', the houses on the above streets would be demolished) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | In Nov and Dec 1962 Resettlement Department staff entered the City several times to hand out 'resettlement notices'. On 28 Dec 1962 dozens of armed police stood guard during the hand-out. | "在十二月二十八日派发“徙置通知书”时，香港英国警察处还派了数十名武装警察布防" (When the 'resettlement notices' were handed out on 28 December, the police also deployed dozens of armed policemen.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | On 1 Jan 1963 the deputy director of the West European Department of the PRC Foreign Ministry, 宋之光, called in the British chargé d'affaires 賈維 to express serious concern. | "一九六三年一月一日我国外交部西欧司副司长宋之光召见英国驻华代办贾维" (On 1 Jan 1963 宋之光, deputy director of the West European Department of our Foreign Ministry, summoned the British chargé d'affaires (賈維).) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | On 7 Jan 1963 the Resettlement Department handed out 'demolition notices' to the residents of these streets. The notices set the demolition of the houses before 8, 12, 14, 19 and 20 Feb 1963. | "要将居民们的房屋分别在二月八日、十二日、十四日、十九日和二十日以前拆除" (the residents' houses were to be demolished before 8, 12, 14, 19 and 20 February respectively) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | The houses under these notices were nearly 200, with more than 2,000 residents. | "这批房屋约近二百间，居民二千余人" (These houses were nearly two hundred, with more than two thousand residents.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | House owners and other residents with property interests refused to accept the resettlement and demolition notices. | "当地的房屋业主和其他有业权利益的居民，在香港英国徙置事务处派发“徙置通知书”和“拆迁通知书”时，纷纷拒绝接受" (the local house owners and other residents with property interests refused to accept the notices) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | Xinhua said that many factory owners, school principals, shopkeepers, doctors and house owners lived in the City, and that they would lose their homes and livelihoods. | "许多居住在城寨的工厂厂主、学校校长、商店店主、医生和房屋业主指出" (many factory owners, school principals, shop owners, doctors and house owners living in the City pointed out) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | Xinhua said that thousands of residents would be left homeless and without a living if the clearance went ahead. | "几千居民将被迫走上流离失所无以为生的绝境" (several thousand residents would be forced into homelessness with no means of living) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | The Wen Wei Po (文匯報) and the Ta Kung Pao (大公報) of 14 Jan 1963 printed the words of the head of the Anti-Demolition and Anti-Resettlement Committee of the Residents of Kowloon (九龍城寨居民聯合反對拆遷委員會; English name as in Peking Review, china-4) and of many resident representatives. | "香港《文汇报》和《大公报》一月十四日还刊登了“九龙城寨居民联合反对拆迁委员会”负责人和许多居民代表的谈话" (Hong Kong's Wen Wei Po and Ta Kung Pao on 14 January printed talks by the head of the committee and many resident representatives.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 18 Jan 1963, page 1 (第1版) | On 17 Jan 1963 the PRC Foreign Ministry protested to the British government. It said that the City is Chinese territory under Chinese jurisdiction, and asked that the clearance decision be cancelled. | "郑重指出九龙城寨是中国的领土，管辖权属于中国，历史上一向如此" (solemnly pointed out that Kowloon Walled City is Chinese territory, jurisdiction belongs to China, and this has always been so in history) |

- **Leads:**
  - Wen Wei Po (文匯報) and Ta Kung Pao (大公報), Hong Kong, 14 Jan 1963: talks of the head of the Anti-Demolition and Anti-Resettlement Committee of the Residents of Kowloon (九龍城寨居民聯合反對拆遷委員會; English name as in Peking Review, china-4) and of resident representatives (cited by the article).
  - Hong Kong Government, 'North-east Kowloon outline development plan' (九龙东北部发展草图计划), May 1960 (cited).
  - Resettlement Department, 'Tung Tau Tsuen Phase 2 resettlement scheme' (东头村第二期徙置计划) notices of 19 Mar 1962 and the demolition notices of 7 Jan 1963 (cited).
  - PRC Foreign Ministry note of protest to the British government, 17 Jan 1963 (cited).
### top-china-3: 大公報 1963-01-18

- **Status:** Partly read on 2026-11-20; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-20. I read the page images (full size) as cropped, enlarged images on 2026-11-20: leaves n2 and n3 were fetched by an earlier run on 2026-11-18, and I fetched leaf n0 on 2026-11-20. I read the whole feature on leaf n3 (第四版) with its subheads (一旦拆屋如何生活, 港府拆遷實無道理, 十一口之家終日不安, 陳樹聞訊憂鬱而死, 借貸殮葬無法還清). The continuation on leaf n2 (第三版) is a poor print: I read part of it, and I could not read the second resident's name and street. The front-page lead (leaf n0) prints the same Xinhua text as china-2; I read only its first columns. Checker (2026-11-20): on leaf n2 the second resident's address reads 社公街七號 (floor faint; added as a fact); her name looks like 梁… and is not sure. Leaf n2 also tells of a shoe hawker (鞋販) with nine children who rented a room from her, and of a woman of 66 at 龍津後街五號: these are not extracted.
- **Citation:** Ta Kung Pao (大公報), Hong Kong, 18 Jan 1963: front-page lead 「港府拆遷九龍城寨 中國向英嚴重抗議」 (p. 1) and 本報專訪 「世代家園不能拆遷 九龍城砦老居民訴述處境」 (p. 4, continued p. 3 as 「城砦老居民反對拆遷」). Internet Archive item NPTKP19630118, https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19630118
- **Class:** primary (A news feature of the time, with the residents' own words. Ta Kung Pao was a pro-Beijing paper, and the feature supports the PRC protest of the same day.)
- **Date of the record:** 1963-01-18
- **Files:**
  - `top-china-3-NPTKP19630118-2a894453.pdf` (in `local/`, not in git): Ta Kung Pao, 18 Jan 1963, whole issue, page-image PDF (6,662,480 bytes); City items on leaves n0, n2 and n3; https://archive.org/download/NPTKP19630118/NPTKP19630118.pdf; 6662480 bytes; SHA-256 `864c282493d17b44cf234c201488a7f173c04e8909f0b7be37d6edff5f3fc65f`; licence: no rights statement on the item (© 大公報) (https://archive.org/details/NPTKP19630118)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|

It reports that the Resettlement Department sent more than 30 people into the City on 19 Mar 1962. (The lead is set in short columns in tiers; the quote runs across two columns.) | "一九六二年三月十九日，香港英國徙置事務處派出三十多人" (On 19 Mar 1962 the British Hong Kong Resettlement Department sent more than thirty people) |

She said that if her house was pulled down she would be ruined. | "未開腔已流淚的老婦胡金有（五十八歲）說：「如果我的屋宇被拆，得個慘字。」" (The old woman 胡金有 (58), in tears before she spoke, said: 'If my house is pulled down, I'll be ruined.') |

| Ta Kung Pao, 18 Jan 1963, NPTKP19630118 leaf n3 (第四版), 本報專訪 「世代家園不能拆遷 九龍城砦老居民訴述處境」 | His widow borrowed from relatives and friends to pay for the funeral and could not repay it (subhead 借貸殮葬無法還清). | "借貸殮葬無法還清" (borrowed for the funeral and cannot repay) |
| Ta Kung Pao, 18 Jan 1963, NPTKP19630118 leaf n3 (第四版), 本報專訪 「世代家園不能拆遷 九龍城砦老居民訴述處境」 | 胡金有's mother, 78, had been a widow since 22 and lived with her.
