### lead-l1158: 许家屯走访九龙城

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-23; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-23.
- **Citation:** Xinhua News Agency (新華社), dispatch from Hong Kong of 7 Aug 1983, 「许家屯走访九龙城」, People's Daily (人民日報), 8 Aug 1983, page 4; transcription on laoziliao.net, https://www.laoziliao.net/rmrb/1983-08-08-4#633432
- **Class:** primary (A Xinhua news dispatch of the day of the event (7 Aug 1983), printed the next day. It is an official Mainland record of the time; the primary tree is right.)
- **Date of the record:** 1983-08-07 (Xinhua dispatch); printed 1983-08-08
- **Files:**
  - `lead-l1158-1983-08-08-4-fbbf0e50.html` (in `local/`, not in git): HTML transcription of page 4 of the People's Daily of 8 Aug 1983 (the City article is the card with id 633432).; https://www.laoziliao.net/rmrb/1983-08-08-4; 31342 bytes; SHA-256 `90796e2ee032620e43ecdab3a3f2d2436768d2cc853e8b07b40cc4984ca9fea5`; licence: Page footer 'CopyRight © 2000 老資料网 Inc.All Rights Reserved.'; text of Xinhua / People's Daily; no reuse licence (https://www.laoziliao.net/rmrb/1983-08-08-4)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| People's Daily (人民日報), 8 Aug 1983, page 4, article 633432 (laoziliao.net transcription) | Xinhua dated its dispatch Hong Kong, 7 August 1983. It says that Xu Jiatun (許家屯), head of the Hong Kong branch of Xinhua, visited Kowloon City (九龍城) that day and greeted the people who lived there. | "新华社香港8月7日电 新华社香港分社社长许家屯，今天走访了位于九龙半岛界限街以北的九龙城" (Xinhua, Hong Kong, 7 Aug: Xu Jiatun, head of the Hong Kong branch of Xinhua, today visited Kowloon City, north of Boundary Street on the Kowloon peninsula.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 8 Aug 1983, page 4, article 633432 (laoziliao.net transcription) | During the visit he asked in detail about how the Walled City was managed and how its residents lived. | "许家屯详细询问了九龙城寨的管理情况和居民的生活情况" (Xu Jiatun asked in detail about the management of Kowloon Walled City and the living conditions of the residents.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 8 Aug 1983, page 4, article 633432 (laoziliao.net transcription) | The dispatch says that the Walled City was managed by a kaifong welfare association (街坊福利事業促進會) that the local residents elected. Xu Jiatun praised this. | "九龙城寨是由当地居民选举出来的街坊福利事业促进会管理时，许家屯表示赞赏" (...that the Walled City is managed by the kaifong welfare association elected by the local residents, Xu Jiatun expressed praise.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 8 Aug 1983, page 4, article 633432 (laoziliao.net transcription) | He showed concern about the many inconveniences of the residents' daily life. | "许家屯还对当地居民在生活上遇到的许多不便表示关切" (Xu Jiatun also expressed concern about the many inconveniences that the local residents meet in their lives.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 8 Aug 1983, page 4, article 633432 (laoziliao.net transcription) | A doctor told him that many people from the Mainland lived in the City, and that they cared about the return of Chinese sovereignty over Hong Kong. | "一位医生对许家屯说，这里居住着许多从大陆来的人士" (A doctor told Xu Jiatun that many people who came from the Mainland live here.) |
| People's Daily (人民日報), 8 Aug 1983, page 4, article 633432 (laoziliao.net transcription) | Xu answered that Hong Kong people must unite with the people who came from the Mainland, and that whoever supports reunification is a patriot. The Xinhua text is shorter here than the Ta Kung Pao text: it leaves out the words 'whoever has come has come, whatever the reason'. | "香港同胞要同大陆来港的人士团结起来。赞成祖国统一的，就是爱国者" (Hong Kong compatriots must unite with the people who came to Hong Kong from the Mainland. Those who support the reunification of the motherland are patriots.) |

- **Leads:**
  - A scan of the printed People's Daily (人民日報) of 8 Aug 1983, page 4 (the repo holds only the transcription).
### lead-l0037: 華僑日報 1947-12-30

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-23; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-23.
- **Citation:** Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報), 「[九]龍城七十一間民房 四十八家必須遷拆 昨日又接通告 居民表示不理」 (特訊), 30 December 1947, local news page (archive.org leaf n3), https://archive.org/details/NPWK19471230/page/n3
- **Class:** primary (A Hong Kong newspaper report of the time (30 Dec 1947) on the events of the day before. It is a Hong Kong Chinese newspaper, thus it fits hk-chinese-press-1947-1984 better than china-official-records-1933-1987 (both are in primary-sources/). Earlier history (the 1947-48 demolitions), long before the peak period.)
- **Date of the record:** 1947-12-30 (events of 29 Dec 1947; eviction order of 22 Dec 1947)
- **Files:**
  - `lead-l0037-n3-013d4caa.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Page image of 華僑日報 30 Dec 1947, leaf n3 (local news), with the article on the 48 demolition notices (about 5.4 MB); https://archive.org/download/NPWK19471230/page/n3.jpg; 5446183 bytes; SHA-256 `ad1180b8e792f0e3ba89eb59abbe5a7b290e0eba184225ce5dd499425630c761`; licence: No licence statement: the archive.org metadata of the item has no rights or licenseurl field; private upload in the 'china-mail' collection; copyright of the newspaper (https://archive.org/details/NPWK19471230)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPWK19471230, leaf n3 (local news page), lower centre, article 龍城七十一間民房 | INFERRED: INFERRED: At about 11 a.m. on 29 December 1947, the residents of the wooden huts in the City again got demolition notices from the Public Works Department (工務局). The date comes from 'yesterday morning' in the paper of 30 December. | "昨晨十一時許，該城內木屋居民復收到本港工務局拆屋通告" (At about 11 a.m. yesterday, the hut residents in the City again received demolition notices from the Public Works Department of this colony.) |
| NPWK19471230, leaf n3 (local news page), lower centre, article 龍城七十一間民房 | The paper says that the demolition affair had gone on for some weeks, but the Hong Kong side had not yet carried it out. | "九龍城拆屋事件，迄今數週，港方仍未付諸實行" (The Kowloon City demolition affair has gone on for some weeks; the Hong Kong side has still not carried it out.) |
| NPWK19471230, leaf n3 (local news page), lower centre, article 龍城七十一間民房 | The notice was the same as the eviction order that the Kowloon magistrate 百里渠 issued on 22 December 1947. | "該通告與本月廿二日九龍裁判署法官百里渠所發驅逐令相同" (The notice is the same as the eviction order issued on the 22nd of this month by magistrate 百里渠 of the Kowloon Magistracy.) |
| NPWK19471230, leaf n3 (local news page), lower centre, article 龍城七十一間民房 | A Chinese official, a British officer of the Public Works Department and a Chinese interpreter took the notices from door to door. | "係由華籍官一名及工務局英籍職員偕同華人譯員一名執行，按戶派送" (It was done by one Chinese official and a British officer of the Public Works Department with one Chinese interpreter, delivering from door to door.) |
| NPWK19471230, leaf n3 (local news page), lower centre, article 龍城七十一間民房 | The paper names 朱沛唐 as the representative of the residents' joint assembly 寶安縣九龍城居民聯合大會 (the name places the City in 寶安縣). | "據該寶安縣九龍城居民聯合大會代表朱沛唐稱" (According to 朱沛唐, representative of the 寶安縣九龍城居民聯合大會.) |
| NPWK19471230, leaf n3 (local news page), lower centre, article 龍城七十一間民房, subhead 口頭通告 | Subhead 'oral notice' (口頭通告): the officials told each household that got a notice to come in person to the Public Works Department the next day (30 Dec) to apply for a new lot in exchange. | "親到工務局申請新地宗交換地點" (come in person to the Public Works Department to apply for a new lot as an exchange site) |
| NPWK19471230, leaf n3 (local news page), lower centre, article 龍城七十一間民房, subhead 口頭通告 | INFERRED: The officials said that otherwise the huts where the residents lived would be pulled down. INFERRED: a stain hides one character before 'pulled down'. | "否則現住之木屋" (otherwise the wooden huts now lived in [would be pulled down]) |
| NPWK19471230, leaf n3 (local news page), lower centre, article 龍城七十一間民房, subhead 口頭通告 | After they got the notices, the residents decided to hold a meeting to discuss what to do. | "已決定開會商討應付辦法" (they have decided to hold a meeting to discuss how to deal with it) |
| NPWK19471230, leaf n3 (local news page), lower centre, article 龍城七十一間民房, subhead 口頭通告 | INFERRED: At about 2 p.m. on 29 Dec the residents' association sent a representative with all the notices to the office of the Special Commissioner for Foreign Affairs for Guangdong and Guangxi (兩廣外交特派員公署) for instructions. | NPWK19471230, leaf n3 (local news page), lower centre, article 龍城七十一間民房, subhead 口頭通告 | The residents got 48 notices in all. | "收得之通告共四十八張" (the notices received were 48 in all) |
| NPWK19471230, leaf n3 (local news page), lower centre, article 龍城七十一間民房, end of the main text | The representatives of the residents said that the households with notices would ignore the order to go to the Public Works Department. | "將置之不理" (they will ignore it) |
| NPWK19471230, leaf n3 (local news page), lower centre, article 龍城七十一間民房, end of the main text | The residents' association 九龍城居民聯合會 issued a statement on the matter on 29 Dec 1947 and asked the Hong Kong Government to reconsider. | "並盼港政府予以考慮云" (and hopes the Hong Kong Government will reconsider) |

INFERRED: the character before 權 (rights) is hard to read; I read 業權 (ownership). | "故須遷出及將房拆卸者，實四十八家" (thus those who must move out and have their houses pulled down are in fact 48 households) |

- **Leads:**
  - Eviction order of 22 Dec 1947 by magistrate 百里渠, Kowloon Magistracy (九龍裁判署), against the hut residents of the City
  - Public Works Department (工務局) demolition notices of 29 Dec 1947 to 48 households of the City (and its list of the 71 households)
  - Statement of 九龍城居民聯合會 of 29 Dec 1947 on the notices
### lead-l0038: 華僑日報 1947-12-09

- **Status:** Partly read on 2026-11-23; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-23. The OCR of this issue is useless, so I found the article by eye on the page images (leaf n3; the same strip shows at the right edge of leaf n4). The article is at the left edge of the page, beside the binding. I read the headline, the subhead and the first two columns in enlarged crops of the full-size scan. The next columns are lost in the black binding gutter of the scan, thus the rest of the article (the results of the survey) is not legible.
- **Citation:** Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報), 「工務局向九龍城新建木屋 調查建築費用 居民忖測當局採補回建築費辦法」 (特訊), 9 December 1947, local news page (archive.org leaf n3), https://archive.org/details/NPWK19471209/page/n3
- **Class:** primary (A Hong Kong newspaper report of the time (9 Dec 1947). It fits hk-chinese-press-1947-1984 better than china-official-records-1933-1987 (both primary). Earlier history (the 1947-48 huts).)
- **Date of the record:** 1947-12-09 (event of 8 Dec 1947)
- **Files:**
  - `lead-l0038-n3-3a177f7b.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Page image of 華僑日報 9 Dec 1947, leaf n3 (local news); the article is at the left edge, partly lost in the binding gutter (about 4.9 MB); https://archive.org/download/NPWK19471209/page/n3.jpg; 4945953 bytes; SHA-256 `409af2b3bfbe6c08198dae35dbbfbaf503b6f47ee66b886fba579ef03e23aba5`; licence: No licence statement: the archive.org metadata of the item has no rights or licenseurl field; private upload in the 'china-mail' collection; copyright of the newspaper (https://archive.org/details/NPWK19471209)

|---|---|---|

The residents guessed that the government would pay back the building costs. | "工務局向九龍城新建木屋 調查建築費用" (Public Works Department surveys the building costs of the new wooden huts in Kowloon City) |
| NPWK19471209, leaf n3 (local news page), left edge at the binding, article 工務局向九龍城新建木屋, subhead | Subhead: the residents guessed that the government would use a method of paying back the building costs. | "居民忖測當局採補回建築費辦法" (Residents guess that the authorities will adopt a method of repaying building costs) |

- **Leads:**
  - Public Works Department (工務局) survey of the building costs of the new huts in the City, 8 Dec 1947 (its register of the huts)

| zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.08, leaf n1 (第二版), centre, article 九龍舊城居民露宿, 中央社香港七日電 | CNA Hong Kong, 7 Jan 1948: the old City, once busy, was now heaps of broken beams and boards. | "刻已化爲斷樑破板" (has now become broken beams and boards) |

- **Leads:**

  - Guangzhou press of Jan 1948 on the student protest march about the City demolitions
  - Hong Kong court record of the charges against the two arrested residents (Jan 1948: inciting disorder, obstructing the police)

| zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.06, leaf n1 (第二版), lower centre, article 港府拆除九龍木屋, 中央社香港五日專電 | 朱沛唐 was the chairman of the 九龍中國居民協會 (an association of the Chinese residents of Kowloon City). | "九龍中國居民協會主席朱沛唐" (朱沛唐, chairman of the 九龍中國居民協會) |
| zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.06, leaf n1 (第二版), lower centre, article 港府拆除九龍木屋, 中央社香港五日專電 | Right after the arrests the government pulled down a number of grass huts and wooden huts in the old City. | "立即拆除九龍舊城內之若干草房木屋" (at once demolished a number of grass huts and wooden huts in the old Kowloon City) |
| zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.06, leaf n1 (第二版), lower centre, article 港府拆除九龍木屋, 中央社香港五日專電 | The workers of the Public Works Department pulled down the first thatched hut at 7:55 a.m. on 5 Jan 1948. | "工務局工人於今晨七時五十五分拆毀第一所茅屋" (Public Works Department workers pulled down the first thatched hut at 7:55 this morning) |
| zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.06, leaf n1 (第二版), lower centre, article 港府拆除九龍木屋, 中央社香港五日專電 | 250 police were used, each with tear-gas grenades and other equipment against trouble. | "共動員警察二百五十名之多、渠等各携有催淚彈" (as many as 250 police were mobilised, each carrying tear-gas grenades) |
| zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.06, leaf n1 (第二版), lower centre, article 港府拆除九龍木屋, 中央社香港四日電 | CNA Hong Kong, 4 Jan 1948: the police special unit (別動隊), about 250 men, was ordered to gather at 6 a.m. the next morning for a secret task. | "香港警察別動隊、全隊約二百五十人" (the Hong Kong police special unit, about 250 men in all) |
| zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.06, leaf n1 (第二版), lower centre, article 港府拆除九龍木屋, 中央社香港四日電 | The secret task was to help pull down the wooden huts of 2,000 Chinese residents in the old City, under the recent eviction order. | zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.06, leaf n1 (第二版), lower centre, article 港府拆除九龍木屋, 中央社香港四日電 | The Public Works Department and the police kept the date of the demolition a close secret, because they feared trouble. | "均以木屋之拆除日期爲最機密之事" (both treated the date of the demolition of the huts as a top secret) |
| zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.06, leaf n1 (第二版), lower centre, article 港府拆除九龍木屋, 中央社香港四日電 | The residents' association (printed 九龍市居民聯合會) had said many times that, if forced to move, the residents would resist, even with bloodshed. | "如被迫遷讓、定將起而反抗、並不惜流血" (if forced to move, they will certainly rise and resist, even at the cost of bloodshed) |

| zhongyang-ribao-1948.01.06, leaf n1 (第二版), lower centre, article 港府拆除九龍木屋, beside: 我外部闢謠 (中央社南京五日電) | CNA Nanjing, 5 Jan 1948: the Foreign Ministry denied a report that Foreign Minister 王世杰 called the eviction an internal question of Hong Kong. - **Leads:**
  - Hong Kong Police records of the operation of 5 Jan 1948 in the City (the special unit of about 250 men)
  - Kowloon Magistracy charge against 朱沛唐 and 劉毅夫, Jan 1948 (obstructing the demolition)

### lead-l0676: 華僑日報 (Wah Kiu Yat Po), 16 January 1948: 「救濟龍城居民省府代表抵港振欵五億擬組會發放今日往醫院慰問傷者」 (OCR headline list)

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-23; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-23.

- **Class:** primary (A Hong Kong newspaper report of the time (16 Jan 1948) on the events of 15 Jan 1948. | NPWK19480116, leaf n3 (local news page), top left of centre, article 救濟龍城居民省府代表抵港 | INFERRED: Staff came with him, among them 侯本珍, and men of the provincial information office (省新聞處) and the social affairs office (社會處). ### lead-l0011-2: 香港工商日報 1946-09-15: 九龍城 治權問題 聲明 (keywords of the headline list)

- **Citation:** Kung Sheung Daily News (香港工商日報), 〈九龍城治權問題 港府發表聲明 認為九龍城全部由英管治〉 (專訊), 15 Sep 1946 (中華民國卅五年九月十五日), 第一張第三頁 (archive.org leaf n2), https://archive.org/details/NPKS19460915/page/n2
- **Class:** primary (A Hong Kong daily's report of the time, with the text of a Hong Kong Government spokesman's statement of 14 Sep 1946. - **Date of the record:** 1946-09-15
- **Files:**
  - `lead-l0011-2-NPKS19460915-d792c2da.pdf` (in `local/`, not in git): Whole issue, image PDF (4.4 MB); https://archive.org/download/NPKS19460915/NPKS19460915.pdf; 4383774 bytes; SHA-256 `c3d891e6179aa982bb8f7daf6c4384b62c0dad1c8e60f64ec0d07a7690647a21`; licence: No rights or licence field in the archive.org metadata (private upload in collection china-mail); the newspaper text is the copyright of its publisher (https://archive.org/details/NPKS19460915)
  - `lead-l0011-2-n2-99de9cc5.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-size page image of leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), with the article; https://archive.org/download/NPKS19460915/page/n2.jpg; 6365319 bytes; SHA-256 `cc7a771bfe5d616d9a50c2031dbdaab62f78e2da9d16694487fde7e8b6bac9ae`; licence: No rights or licence field in the archive.org metadata (private upload in collection china-mail); the newspaper text is the copyright of its publisher (https://archive.org/details/NPKS19460915)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPKS19460915, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 九龍城治權問題 港府發表聲明, headline | The headline says that the Hong Kong Government made a statement on the question of jurisdiction over Kowloon City, and that it holds that the whole City is under British rule. | "九龍城治權問題 港府發表聲明 認為九龍城全部由英管治" (The question of jurisdiction over Kowloon City: the Hong Kong Government issues a statement; holds that Kowloon City is wholly under British rule) |
| NPKS19460915, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 九龍城治權問題 港府發表聲明, col. 1 | INFERRED: A Hong Kong Government spokesman made the statement "yesterday". The paper is of 15 Sep 1946, thus the day is 14 Sep 1946 (INFERRED from 昨日). | "港府發言人昨日宣稱" (a Hong Kong Government spokesman stated yesterday) |
| NPKS19460915, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 九龍城治權問題 港府發表聲明, col. 1-3 | The spokesman said that the local Chinese papers had lately printed articles that misunderstood the status of Kowloon City. The articles held that the City differs in some way from the other places of the New Territories. | "本港中文報近曾登載論文，表示有等人士對九龍城之地位，有所誤辨" (the Chinese papers of this colony have lately printed articles showing that some people misunderstand the status of Kowloon City) |
| NPKS19460915, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 九龍城治權問題 港府發表聲明, col. 3-5 | The articles held that Kowloon City is Chinese territory, and that a treaty kept the land inside the City wall as Chinese territory. | "于各論文力認九龍城為中國領土，且稱九龍城城垣內之土地" (the articles insist that Kowloon City is Chinese territory, and say that the land inside the walls of Kowloon City ...) |
| NPKS19460915, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 九龍城治權問題 港府發表聲明, second block, col. 1-4 | The spokesman said that under the Peking convention of 1898 China and Britain agreed a lease of 99 years. It moved the British boundary north to the Deep Bay and Mirs Bay frontier. | "根據一八九八年北京條約，中英兩國已同意訂立一為期九十九年之租借協約" (under the Peking convention of 1898, China and Britain agreed to a lease of 99 years) |
| NPKS19460915, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 九龍城治權問題 港府發表聲明, second block, col. 4-6 | The spokesman said that the convention did not state that Kowloon City was left out of the lease, and that its wording does not fit such an intention. | "未有聲明九龍城不包括在內" (it did not state that Kowloon City was not included) |
| NPKS19460915, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 九龍城治權問題 港府發表聲明, second block, col. 7-9 | As the spokesman read the convention, the Chinese officials then stationed in the City could keep their jurisdiction only so long as it did not conflict with the military needs of the defence of Hong Kong. | "除非對保衞香港之軍事需要有不適合時，當時駐九龍城之中國官吏，得繼續行使法權" (unless it conflicted with the military needs of the defence of Hong Kong, the Chinese officials then stationed in Kowloon City could continue to exercise jurisdiction) |
| NPKS19460915, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 九龍城治權問題 港府發表聲明, lower block, col. 1-4 | The spokesman argued that if the City were outside the leased land, the convention would not need a clause on the jurisdiction of the Chinese officials there, because jurisdiction belongs to the sovereign state. | "若九龍城不包括在租借地內，則當然無規定駐該地之中國官吏之法權之必要" (if Kowloon City were not included in the leased territory, there would of course be no need to provide for the jurisdiction of the Chinese officials stationed there) |
| NPKS19460915, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 九龍城治權問題 港府發表聲明, lower block, col. 4-7 | The spokesman said that from 1 July 1898, when the convention took effect, Kowloon City became part of the extended British territory, under British sovereignty, for the term of the lease. | "自一八九八年七月一日北京條約發生效力起，于租借期內，九龍城已變為英國之擴展領土之一部" (from 1 July 1898, when the Peking convention took effect, for the term of the lease, Kowloon City became part of the extended British territory) |
| NPKS19460915, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 九龍城治權問題 港府發表聲明, lower block, col. 8-11 | The spokesman said that in 1899 the jurisdiction of the Chinese officials in the City conflicted with the military needs of the defence of Hong Kong, and thus it ended. | "一八九九年，中國官吏在九龍城內行使法權已與保衛香港之軍事需要不相吻合" (in 1899 the exercise of jurisdiction by Chinese officials in Kowloon City conflicted with the military needs of the defence of Hong Kong) |
| NPKS19460915, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 九龍城治權問題 港府發表聲明, last block | The statement ends that for almost fifty years the British authorities have used full jurisdiction in the City, the same as in the rest of the New Territories. | "迄今將有五十年，英國當局在九龍城內，業已行使全部法權，一如在其他新界各地" (for almost fifty years now the British authorities have exercised full jurisdiction in Kowloon City, as in the other places of the New Territories) |

- **Leads:**
  - The English text of the Hong Kong Government spokesman's statement of 14 Sep 1946 on the status of Kowloon City (INFERRED to be in the English papers of 15 Sep 1946, for example the China Mail or the South China Morning Post).
  - The Hong Kong Chinese press articles of early September 1946 that claimed Kowloon City as Chinese territory (named, not identified, in the statement; Kung Sheung Daily News, 15 Sep 1946, leaf n2).
### lead-l0012-2: 香港工商日報 1946-09-18: 我在九龍城設治問題 外交部發言人 (keywords of the headline list)

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-24; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-25.
- **Citation:** Central News Agency (中央社), Nanking, 17 Sep 1946, in Kung Sheung Daily News (香港工商日報), 〈我在九龍城設治問題 外交部發言人發表談話〉, 18 Sep 1946 (中華民國卅五年九月十八日), 第一張第三頁 (archive.org leaf n2), https://archive.org/details/NPKS19460918/page/n2
- **Class:** primary (A news report of the time (a Central News Agency dispatch of 17 Sep 1946) with the words of a Chinese Foreign Ministry spokesman. The words are an official Chinese statement, thus the folder china-official-records-1933-1987 fits.)
- **Date of the record:** 1946-09-17
- **Files:**
  - `lead-l0012-2-NPKS19460918-351b90d8.pdf` (in `local/`, not in git): Whole issue, image PDF (4.1 MB); https://archive.org/download/NPKS19460918/NPKS19460918.pdf; 4078472 bytes; SHA-256 `9023f2720f7e5ca0d1c5af30eed3c9c278f4568c862acc3ce0626e4a303aca56`; licence: No rights or licence field in the archive.org metadata (private upload in collection china-mail); the newspaper text is the copyright of its publisher (https://archive.org/details/NPKS19460918)
  - `lead-l0012-2-n2-f1f41705.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-size page image of leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), with the article; https://archive.org/download/NPKS19460918/page/n2.jpg; 5726028 bytes; SHA-256 `bc5af7242dbe41ef1f6e5324a638b7bac6e905819e0084a165dc3f23b8573252`; licence: No rights or licence field in the archive.org metadata (private upload in collection china-mail); the newspaper text is the copyright of its publisher (https://archive.org/details/NPKS19460918)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPKS19460918, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 我在九龍城設治問題 外交部發言人發表談話, headline | The headline says that the Foreign Ministry spokesman spoke on the question of Chinese administration in Kowloon City. | "我在九龍城設治問題 外交部發言人發表談話" (On the question of our setting up administration in Kowloon City: the Foreign Ministry spokesman makes a statement) |
| NPKS19460918, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 我在九龍城設治問題 外交部發言人發表談話, col. 1 | The spokesman was 何鳳山, head of the Information Department of the Foreign Ministry. He answered reporters in Nanking on 17 Sep 1946. | "【南京十七日中央社電】外部發言人情報司長何鳳山" ([Central News Agency, Nanking, 17th] Foreign Ministry spokesman and head of the Information Department 何鳳山) |
| NPKS19460918, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 我在九龍城設治問題 外交部發言人發表談話, col. 2-3 | He answered the recent statement of the Hong Kong Government spokesman, who denied that the area inside Kowloon City is still Chinese territory and that China now has jurisdiction there. | "否認九龍城內區域仍為中國領土，及中國目前在該區行使治權一節" (denied that the area inside Kowloon City is still Chinese territory, and that China at present exercises jurisdiction there) |
| NPKS19460918, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 我在九龍城設治問題 外交部發言人發表談話, col. 4-5 | The reporters' question quotes the Hong Kong claim that Chinese jurisdiction in the City ended because it conflicted with British military needs. The question gives the year 1898; the Hong Kong statement printed on 15 Sep 1946 (lead-l0011-2) gives 1899. | "一八九八年中國官吏在九龍城內之治權，已因與英方軍事需要有所抵觸，宣告中止" (in 1898 the jurisdiction of the Chinese officials in Kowloon City was declared ended, because it conflicted with British military needs) |
| NPKS19460918, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 我在九龍城設治問題 外交部發言人發表談話, col. 6-8 | He quoted the 1898 Convention for the Extension of Hong Kong Territory: the Chinese officials then in the City can continue their duties there, provided they do not hinder the defence of Hong Kong. | "現在九龍城內駐紮之中國官員，仍可在城內各司其事，惟不得與保衛香港之武備，有所妨礙" (the Chinese officials now stationed in Kowloon City may continue to perform their duties there, provided they do not interfere with the defence of Hong Kong) |
| NPKS19460918, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 我在九龍城設治問題 外交部發言人發表談話, col. 8-9 | He said that because the convention lets Chinese officials continue their duties, China sends officials into Kowloon, and it has never given up its jurisdiction in the City. | "則我國自必派官員進入九龍，對九龍城內治權，從未放棄也" (then our country naturally sends officials into Kowloon, and has never given up jurisdiction in Kowloon City) |
| NPKS19460918, leaf n2 (printed 第一張第三頁), article 我在九龍城設治問題 外交部發言人發表談話, col. 10-12 | Asked if the two sides read the treaty differently, he said that under international law a treaty is read in favour of the state that bears the obligation. China was that state in the 1898 lease. | "應作有利於承擔義務國之解釋，而我國在一八九八年中英租借九龍條約中，為承擔義務之國家" (it should be interpreted in favour of the state bearing the obligation, and in the 1898 lease of Kowloon our country was the state bearing the obligation) |

- **Leads:**
  - The Central News Agency (中央社) dispatch of 17 Sep 1946 with the statement of 何鳳山, as other Chinese papers of 18 Sep 1946 printed it (for example 華僑日報 or 星島日報 of that day), to check the wording.
### lead-l0013-1: 廣州掀起排英怒潮 - 援助九龍城難胞 (Toronto 醒華日報 clipping, 香港英合眾社 dispatch)

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-24; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-25.
- **Citation:** United Press, Hong Kong (香港英合衆社), 〈廣州掀起排英怒潮 要洗雪港九被佔百年恥辱 滬亦醞釀響應此正義運動〉 (kicker 援助九龍城難胞), Shing Wah Daily News (醒華日報), Toronto, 16 Jan 1948, p. 2 (第二頁); clipping in the Richard Charles Lee Canada-Hong Kong Library, University of Toronto, https://collections.library.utoronto.ca/view/rclc-hkl:xhrb_article0138_B11_1948-01-16
- **Class:** primary (A wire report of the time (16 Jan 1948) in an overseas Chinese paper. It is press, not an official record: it fits a press folder better than china-official-records-1933-1987 (same tree, primary). Its subject is the reaction in Canton and Shanghai to the Kowloon City evictions, not life in the City.)
- **Date of the record:** 1948-01-16
- **Files:**
  - `lead-l0013-1-default-d315fd5e.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Scan of the clipping (6420 x 4500 px, IIIF image service); https://iiif.library.utoronto.ca/image/v2/rclc-hkl:xhrb_article0138_B11_1948-01-16/full/full/0/default.jpg; 4595715 bytes; SHA-256 `6ffb4cffe5baa1aa980b1d32c7744724710d63c0b4bb9b29e023537ee4fec4a3`; licence: Rights Information on the item page: 'Digital reproduction of this material has been granted to the University of Toronto Libraries by the copyright holder. These images may be used for personal and educational/research purposes.' Other uses need permission. (https://collections.library.utoronto.ca/view/rclc-hkl:xhrb_article0138_B11_1948-01-16)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| Shing Wah Daily News, 16 Jan 1948, p. 2 (第二頁), clipping xhrb_article0138, headline | The kicker of the headline is "help the Kowloon City refugees"; the headline says that an anti-British wave rose in Canton. | "援助九龍城難胞 廣州掀起排英怒潮" (Help the Kowloon City refugees: an anti-British wave rises in Canton) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 16 Jan 1948, p. 2 (第二頁), clipping xhrb_article0138, col. 1-2 | United Press reported from Hong Kong on 16 Jan 1948 that British police pulled down the huts of the old Kowloon City, wounded homeless people and jailed Chinese. This set off a strong anti-British movement among the people and students of South China. | "英警強拆九龍舊城茅屋，擊傷無家可歸及監禁華人事，今日已激起華南民衆學生發生強烈之排英運動" (the British police forcibly pulled down the huts of old Kowloon City, wounded the homeless and jailed Chinese; today this has stirred a strong anti-British movement among South China people and students) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 16 Jan 1948, p. 2 (第二頁), clipping xhrb_article0138, col. 2-3 | More than 5,000 angry students surrounded the British Consulate-General in Canton. The crowd set it on fire and it burned almost completely. | "憤激學生五千餘名，排隊包圍英國駐廣州總領事館，此領事館隨為憤激民衆放火焚燒殆盡" (more than 5,000 angry students lined up and surrounded the British Consulate-General in Canton; the angry crowd then set it on fire and it burned almost entirely) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 16 Jan 1948, p. 2 (第二頁), clipping xhrb_article0138, col. 3-4 | The British vice-consul 布勒維爾 was beaten and slightly hurt. The crowd burned the residences of the consul-general 蘭那赫爾 and his assistants. | "英副領事布勒維爾氏被毆受微傷，總領事蘭那赫爾氏及其助手等官邸均為民衆付之一炬" (British vice-consul 布勒維爾 was beaten and slightly hurt; the residences of consul-general 蘭那赫爾 and his assistants were all burned by the crowd) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 16 Jan 1948, p. 2 (第二頁), clipping xhrb_article0138, col. 4-6 | The wife of the consulate's postal officer 摩理 was beaten by the crowd before she was rescued. Police got the consul-general out. A British missionary couple (named as the Rev. 約翰威廉) was missing. | "總領事則為警察保護脫險，英教士約翰威廉牧師夫婦失踪" (the consul-general escaped under police protection; the British missionary the Rev. 約翰威廉 and his wife were missing) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 16 Jan 1948, p. 2 (第二頁), clipping xhrb_article0138, col. 6-8 | In 沙面 (the foreign concession in Canton) the British information office, shipping company offices, other British company buildings and British homes were damaged, and the British flag was pulled down. | "沙面英國情報社，船務公司辦事處及其他之英人公司樓宇，或英僑住宅物業，皆受輕重損失" (in Shameen the British information office, shipping company offices and other British company buildings or British residents' homes all suffered damage, heavy or light) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 16 Jan 1948, p. 2 (第二頁), clipping xhrb_article0138, col. 8-10 | The Canton military authorities sent troops to protect British residents. The troops dispersed the crowd, and the consul-general and consular staff were taken to the US consul's residence for protection. | "英總領事及一般領館官員則送入美領事官邸予以暫時保護" (the British consul-general and the consular staff were taken into the US consul's residence for temporary protection) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 16 Jan 1948, p. 2 (第二頁), clipping xhrb_article0138, later columns | The dispatch says that students in Shanghai petitioned the Nanking government to send troops to protect the homeless refugees of Kowloon. | "要求政府派兵保護九龍無家可歸之難胞" (asking the government to send troops to protect the homeless refugees of Kowloon) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 16 Jan 1948, p. 2 (第二頁), clipping xhrb_article0138, AP item (上海美聯社十六日電) | An AP dispatch from Shanghai of 16 Jan 1948 says that the Shanghai city government added police guards at the British consulate after the Canton riot. | "上海市政府為着駐滬英領事館安全計，今日加派警察駐守英領館" (for the safety of the British consulate in Shanghai, the Shanghai city government today sent more police to guard it) |
