| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), col. 1-2 | The report is dated 13 Jan 1948, from Hong Kong. It says that about 200 armed Hong Kong police went into Kowloon City at dawn on 12 Jan 1948 to clear the remaining wooden huts. | "一月十三日香港航訊：香港政府武裝警察約二百人，昨十二日于晨光熹微之際，開進九龍城" (13 Jan, Hong Kong air-mail report: about 200 armed Hong Kong Government police yesterday, the 12th, at first light, went into Kowloon City) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), col. 2-3 | Several hundred residents resisted with stones for about half an hour. The police used guns and tear gas. | "數百居民以石子作武器，起而抗拒，互戰達半小時，警察使用槍彈與催淚彈" (several hundred residents took up stones as weapons and resisted; the fight lasted half an hour; the police used bullets and tear gas) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), col. 3-4 | Six residents were badly hurt and taken to hospital; ten to twenty more had light injuries. One British policeman was hurt on the bridge of the nose, and one on a hand. (The words 一手受損 can also mean that the same man hurt a hand.) | "六名居民重傷入院，輕傷者十餘廿人，英警一人鼻樑受損，一手受損" (six residents badly hurt and in hospital, ten-odd to twenty lightly hurt; one British policeman's nose injured, one hand injured) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), col. 5-8 | The office of the Special Commissioner for Foreign Affairs in Hong Kong said that it had had no notice from Hong Kong before the 12 Jan action. 郭德華 hurried back from Canton that night. | "十二日之事，本處事前並未接到港方通知" (of the events of the 12th, this office received no notice from Hong Kong beforehand) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), col. 9-10 | From 4 pm on 12 Jan 1948 the police sealed off Kowloon City and let no residents or others in or out. | "九龍城由昨下午四時起，港警察實行予以封鎖，不許居民或各方面人士進出" (from 4 pm yesterday the Hong Kong police sealed off Kowloon City and did not allow residents or anyone else in or out) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), sub-head 武裝警察大隊出動 | The police force was about 200 uniformed men and some dozens of plain-clothes detectives, armed with pistols, a few rifles, tear gas, gas masks and rattan shields. Senior police officers led them. | "港方警察約二百人，配合便衣警探數十名各配槍實彈，少數持長槍，催淚彈，防毒面具，藤牌等" (about 200 Hong Kong police, with some dozens of plain-clothes detectives, each with loaded pistols, a few with rifles, tear gas, gas masks, rattan shields and so on) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), sub-head 武裝警察大隊出動 | A Public Works Department officer led some dozens of demolition workers. At 6.50 am the police arrived in front of Kowloon City in twelve riot vans and police cars, with two ambulances behind. | "于晨六時五十分，分乘衝鋒車，警車十二輛，駛至九龍城前，另有十字車兩輛隨後" (at 6.50 am, in twelve riot vans and police cars, they drove to the front of Kowloon City, with two ambulances following) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), sub-head 武裝警察大隊出動 | The police tried to enter by the East Gate (東門). The residents had heard of it in advance and gathered there; they beat gongs and washbasins as a signal. | "由率領者分隊欲自東門入城，居民似已預先聞訊，亦在東門一帶集結" (the leaders split the police into squads to enter the city by the East Gate; the residents seemed to have heard in advance and also gathered near the East Gate) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), sub-head 武裝警察大隊出動 | The residents beat gongs or washbasins to call each other together. | "鳴鑼或敲面盆等為號，紛紛集中" (they beat gongs or washbasins as a signal and gathered in crowds) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), sub-head 武裝警察大隊出動 | When the police came near the old city wall foundations, the residents threw stones at them. The police used their rattan shields for the first time and took cover behind houses. | "當警察繼續前進將至原日城基時，居民即以石子作武器" (when the police moved on and came near the old wall foundations, the residents took up stones as weapons) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), sub-head 開槍掃射放催淚彈 | The fight was hardest at the paper-burning pavilion (字紙亭), which changed hands six times. Strong men threw stones in front, and women and children carried stones to them from the hill. | "特別在字紙亭之爭奪，至為激烈，互相進退凡六次之多" (especially the fight for the 字紙亭 was most fierce; the two sides advanced and fell back six times) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), sub-head 開槍掃射放催淚彈 | Women and children carried stones from the hill to the back of the pavilion for the men. | "壯者為前鋒，婦人與孺子則輸送石子，自山上到亭後" (the strong men were in front; the women and children carried stones from the hill to behind the pavilion) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), sub-head 開槍掃射放催淚彈 | The police first fired warning shots; the residents did not give way, and the police were ordered to fire. Residents said about a hundred shots were fired, from pistols and rifles. | "事後據居民談：大約可有一百响，其中包括短槍及步槍聲音" (afterwards residents said there were about a hundred shots, including pistol and rifle fire) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1), last columns | The police threw five tear-gas grenades (another account says seven). The gas filled the area of the paper-burning pavilion by the East Gate; the wounded fell, and those who could still stand fled up the hill. | "連擲五枚（一說七枚）遂使東門字紙亭一帶催淚烟滿佈" (threw five in a row (another account says seven), so that tear-gas smoke filled the area of the 字紙亭 at the East Gate) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 20, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情 (clipping xhrb_article0893-1) and Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, p. 2, continuation (xhrb_article0893-2) | Police in gas masks went in and pulled down fewer than ten temporary wooden huts near the paper-burning pavilion by the East Gate. More than a hundred residents left homeless by the earlier demolition had put them up again three or four days before; most were women and children. | "將為數不過十間臨時重搭之木屋折毀，此等木屋，均在東門字紙亭附近" (pulled down the temporary rebuilt wooden huts, fewer than ten in number; these huts were all near the 字紙亭 at the East Gate) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, p. 2, continuation (xhrb_article0893-2) | By 9 am all the huts were down. Afterwards the residents, in tears, picked up their quilts and clothes. | "故未到九時，所有木屋已折毀一空。居民于事後含淚檢拾棉被衣物" (thus before 9 o'clock all the wooden huts had been pulled down; afterwards the residents in tears picked up their quilts and clothes) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 26 Jan 1948, p. 2, continuation (xhrb_article0893-2) | The paper-burning pavilion had become the place where gifts for the residents were collected. Its brick wall was also pulled down that day, but the workers stopped near the portrait of Sun Yat-sen (孫總理) posted on it, and the national flag still flew in the City. | "日來成為慰勞品集中處之字紙亭，本有磚牆一處，昨日亦被折毀" (the 字紙亭, which in recent days had become the collecting point for relief gifts, had a brick wall; it too was pulled down yesterday) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 21, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（二） (xhrb_article0893-3), sub-head 廿餘受傷六名入院 | Part 2 lists six badly hurt residents taken by the police to Kowloon Hospital: 孫日 (male, 48, left eye), 張忠武 (male, 28, head), 張炳林 (male, 29, abdomen), 劉芳怡 (female, 36, back), 梁三妹 (female, 24, knee) and 梁昌 (male, 24, abdomen and hand). | "其中六名重傷，由警方送入九龍醫院救治" (six of them badly hurt, sent by the police to Kowloon Hospital for treatment) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 21, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（二） (xhrb_article0893-3), sub-head 廿餘受傷六名入院 | About twenty residents in all were hurt by bullets or fragments. | "城內居民於紛亂中為槍彈或彈片等所傷者，約廿餘衆" (the city residents hurt by bullets or fragments in the confusion numbered about twenty-odd) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 21, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（二） (xhrb_article0893-3), sub-head 廿餘受傷六名入院 | Two more, less badly hurt, would not go to hospital: 彭九仔 (male, 10, mouth) and 李玉 (female, 21, chest). | "彭九仔（男，十歲，傷口），（二）李玉（女，廿一歲，傷胸）" (彭九仔 (male, 10 years, injured mouth), (2) 李玉 (female, 21 years, injured chest)) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 21, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（二） (xhrb_article0893-3), sub-head 廿餘受傷六名入院 | The residents first did not want the wounded taken to Kowloon Hospital and hired a taxi to take them to the 廣華醫院 (a hospital). The 廣華醫院 refused them under its rule for accident injuries, thus a government ambulance took them to Kowloon Hospital. | "曾一度自請的士欲送往廣華醫院，但該院以屬於意外受傷，依例拒絕收留" (they once hired a taxi themselves to take them to Kwong Wah Hospital, but the hospital, since these were accident injuries, refused them by its rule) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 21, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（二） (xhrb_article0893-3), sub-head 封鎖城區禁止往來 | After the shooting most residents withdrew up the hill and stayed there until evening, looking down on the city where they had lived. | "流血案發生後，居民多退至山上，迄晚依然" (after the bloodshed most residents withdrew up the hill, and were still there by evening) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 21, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（二） (xhrb_article0893-3), sub-head 封鎖城區禁止往來 | About twenty European police stayed in the City from morning to night. After 4 pm no one could go in or out freely, and detectives questioned people moving about inside. | "武裝之警察（西警約廿人），自晨迄至入夜，仍分佈城內" (armed police (about twenty Western officers) stayed spread through the city from morning into the night) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 21, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（二） (xhrb_article0893-3), sub-head 封鎖城區禁止往來 | The police worked in shifts of about 200 men. The first shift came from the Kowloon divisions, gathered at Hung Hom (紅磡), and left at about noon; other shifts followed, and some men came from Hong Kong Island. All police leave in the colony was stopped that day. | "最初出發者為九龍各區警員，先集中於紅磡" (the first to set out were police of the various Kowloon districts, who gathered first at Hung Hom) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 21, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（二） (xhrb_article0893-3), sub-head 催淚彈嗅及氣味涕淚齊下 | The tear gas still hung in the City at dusk. In the morning the reporter could not stand in the worst area for two minutes. In the evening the police spread bleaching powder to clear the smell. | "迄晚，警方運到漂粉一批，分洒各處，藉以清除藥味" (by evening the police brought a lot of bleaching powder and scattered it about to clear the chemical smell) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 21, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（二） (xhrb_article0893-3), sub-head 催淚彈嗅及氣味涕淚齊下 | Only late in the day could the residents cook. They cooked on makeshift stoves under the broken City wall. The handing out of rice and bread had stopped. | "多在崩頹之城垣下，結臨時爐灶為炊" (mostly under the crumbled city wall, building temporary stoves to cook) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 21, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（二） (xhrb_article0893-3), sub-head 居民露宿城內誓死不願離去 | INFERRED: The police left at 6.30 pm. That night the residents put up a flagpole more than two zhang (about 6-7 m, INFERRED from 二丈) high in front of the paper-burning pavilion. They posted portraits of Sun Yat-sen and his couplet on the wall and planned to raise and lower the flag each morning and evening. | "植下長逾二丈之旗桿一支" (set up a flagpole more than two zhang long) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 21, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（二） (xhrb_article0893-3), sub-head 居民露宿城內誓死不願離去 | Several hundred residents prepared to sleep on mats on the ground in the City that night. They said they would stay whatever happened. | "在城內準備席地露宿之居民，仍有數百人" (the residents preparing to sleep in the open on mats in the city still numbered several hundred) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, p. 2, continuation (xhrb_article0893-4) | The police had taken away most of the fir planks and timber that the residents used to build huts, but they slept in the open in the City. At about 7 pm two squads of police with rattan shields and steel helmets patrolled the City, but residents could come and go freely. | "彼等用以蓋搭木屋之杉板木料，多已被警方搬去，但彼等均寧露宿城內" (the fir planks and timber they used to build huts had mostly been taken away by the police, but they all preferred to sleep in the open in the city) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, p. 2, continuation (xhrb_article0893-4), sub-head 居民昨晚發告急書 | That night the residents sent out an urgent appeal "of blood and tears" to people in Hong Kong and China. It told how the police shot the residents, and asked government bodies and public groups for help. | "又九龍城居民昨晚發出血淚告急書，分向本港及國內同胞呼籲" (also last night the Kowloon City residents issued an urgent appeal in blood and tears, addressed to compatriots in Hong Kong and in China) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 28 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 22, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（三） (xhrb_article0893-5), col. 1-3 | Part 3 says that the residents' representative 胡金 reported at once to the office of the Special Commissioner for Foreign Affairs in Hong Kong. Then he went by car to Bao'an County (寶安縣) to report to the county government and the county support committee. | "居民代表胡金，於慘劇發生後，立即赴兩廣外交特派員公署駐港辦事處報告" (the residents' representative 胡金, after the tragedy, went at once to report to the Hong Kong office of the Liang-Kuang Special Commissioner for Foreign Affairs) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 28 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 22, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（三） (xhrb_article0893-5), sub-head 郭德華急返港 | The commissioner's office sent its secretary 吳忠信 to the scene to investigate and to visit the wounded. 郭德華 returned from Canton that evening. | "外部辦事處昨日下午，並派秘書吳忠信前往肇事地點，調查一切，同時慰問傷者" (yesterday afternoon the Foreign Ministry office also sent secretary 吳忠信 to the scene to investigate, and to visit the wounded) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 28 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 22, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（三） (xhrb_article0893-5), sub-head 郭德華急返港 | The residents said that they had heard of the action the night before. They met that night and the next morning and agreed to hold out and not leave. | "當晚及昨晨均開會討論，一致決定堅苦支持，決不離開一步" (that night and yesterday morning they met and decided together to hold out, and not to leave a single step) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 28 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 22, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（三） (xhrb_article0893-5), sub-head 郭德華急返港 | The residents' body was listing the families of the wounded and giving them certificates. It planned to give them money and rice from the relief collected outside, and compensation if anyone died. | "現下正調查傷者之家屬，給予憑證，並擬在外界募賑之款物中，撥給款項及白米" (they are now checking the families of the wounded and giving them certificates, and plan to give money and white rice from the relief collected outside) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 28 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 22, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（三） (xhrb_article0893-5), col. after the sub-head | The relief mission of the Kwangtung Provincial Assembly sent only its secretary 鄭維邦 that day. Its 57 large bags of rice were left with 曾生 to keep, because they were not allowed into the City. | "白米五十七大包，交曾生暫保管，因昨日情形，未允許運入城內也" (57 large bags of white rice, left with 曾生 for safe keeping, because, given yesterday's situation, they were not allowed into the city) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 28 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 22, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（三） (xhrb_article0893-5), col. after the sub-head | A member of the mission said that it would rouse the 35 million people of the province to back the government's negotiations. | "誓發動全省三千五百萬同胞以至全國同胞，為政府交涉後盾" (we vow to rouse the 35 million compatriots of the whole province, and even the whole nation, to back the government's negotiations) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 28 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 22, p. 1, 特稿 九龍城流血慘案詳情（三） (xhrb_article0893-5), sub-head 外交處表示 已電京報告 | The commissioner's office told the Central News Agency (中央社) that it had no notice of the 12 Jan action, that it had cabled Nanking and Canton, and that at least five people were known to be hurt. | "據報受傷者至少已有五人，確實數目，仍待調查" (by reports at least five people have been injured; the exact number is still under investigation) |

- **Leads:**
  - The Kowloon City residents' urgent appeal (血淚告急書) of the night of 12 Jan 1948 to compatriots in Hong Kong and China (Shing Wah Daily News, 27 Jan 1948, p. 2).
### lead-l0013-3: 九龍城事件 (Toronto 醒華日報 clipping)

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-24; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-25.
- **Citation:** Shing Wah Daily News (醒華日報), Toronto, editorial (社論) 〈九龍城事件〉, Thursday 15 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 11, p. 1, continued on p. 2; with the news item 〈港警再拆居民茅屋 英大使已允向倫敦轉達我外部抗議〉 (重慶廣播訊, 13 Jan), p. 2; clipping in the Richard Charles Lee Canada-Hong Kong Library, University of Toronto, https://collections.library.utoronto.ca/view/rclc-hkl:xhrb_article0892
- **Class:** primary (An editorial of the time (15 Jan 1948) in an overseas Chinese paper, with a radio news item. It is opinion and news of the time, kind primary, but its history of the City (1899-1947) is background (kind secondary). It is press, not an official record: a press folder fits better than china-official-records-1933-1987 (same tree).)
- **Date of the record:** 1948-01-15
- **Files:**
  - `lead-l0013-3-default-51374887.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Scan of part 1 (editorial, p. 1) (8580 x 4500 px, IIIF image service); https://iiif.library.utoronto.ca/image/v2/rclc-hkl:xhrb_article0892-1_B11_1948-01-15/full/full/0/default.jpg; 6545680 bytes; SHA-256 `831fb85cc972c0c015c08530f0cdd08cf3b1a39cffb6a85ec489586e93096ec8`; licence: Rights Information on the item page: 'Digital reproduction of this material has been granted to the University of Toronto Libraries by the copyright holder. These images may be used for personal and educational/research purposes.' Other uses need permission. (https://collections.library.utoronto.ca/view/rclc-hkl:xhrb_article0892)
  - `lead-l0013-3-default-6b6385c0.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Scan of part 2 (end of the editorial and the news item, p. 2) (5639 x 2400 px, IIIF image service); https://iiif.library.utoronto.ca/image/v2/rclc-hkl:xhrb_article0892-2_B11_1948-01-15/full/full/0/default.jpg; 1839437 bytes; SHA-256 `b3e553cb3b30523092088542af55c019b7161785ad0b5186fe83e976eb3b71af`; licence: Rights Information on the item page: 'Digital reproduction of this material has been granted to the University of Toronto Libraries by the copyright holder. These images may be used for personal and educational/research purposes.' Other uses need permission. (https://collections.library.utoronto.ca/view/rclc-hkl:xhrb_article0892)
- **Note:** its 3 secondary facts (narration, later captions) are in the secondary twin, [`../../secondary-sources/china-official-records-1933-1987/SOURCE.md`](../../secondary-sources/china-official-records-1933-1987/SOURCE.md).

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 11, p. 1, 社論 九龍城事件 (clipping xhrb_article0892, part 1), col. 1-2 | The editorial says that for days the Hong Kong Government had used force to pull down the wooden huts of Kowloon City residents, with tear gas and gunfire, and that on the morning of the 12th (12 Jan 1948) this became a massacre of Chinese. | "拆毀九龍城居民木屋，竟用催淚彈及開鎗強制執行，而於十二日晨演成屠殺我華人慘劇矣" (pulled down the wooden huts of Kowloon City residents, even using tear gas and gunfire, and on the morning of the 12th it became a massacre of our Chinese) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 11, p. 1, 社論 九龍城事件 (clipping xhrb_article0892, part 1), col. 3-4 | The editorial cites the paper's radio news from Nanking and Chungking: the Chinese government was making a second serious protest to the British ambassador. | 「政府正向英駐華大使作第二次嚴重抗議」 ("the government is making a second serious protest to the British ambassador to China") |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 11, p. 1, 社論 九龍城事件 (clipping xhrb_article0892, part 1), middle columns | The editorial says that the Hong Kong Government issued its notice on 27 Nov 1947. It cited Ordinance No. 40 of 1932, section 9(1): no one may occupy land without a valid licence of the Public Works Department or the District Office. | "係於去年十一月廿七日發出通告，以根據一九三二年第四十條則例第九款第一節" (it issued a notice on 27 November last year, on the basis of section 9(1) of Ordinance No. 40 of 1932) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 11, p. 1, 社論 九龍城事件 (clipping xhrb_article0892, part 1), middle columns | The notice ordered the more than 2,000 residents of Kowloon City to move out within two weeks. | "而限令九龍城之二千餘居民在兩週內遷出" (and ordered the more than 2,000 residents of Kowloon City to move out within two weeks) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 11, p. 1, 社論 九龍城事件 (clipping xhrb_article0892, part 1), middle columns | The residents asked 郭德華, the Special Commissioner for Foreign Affairs in Kwangtung and Kwangsi, for help. He went to Nanking on 4 December 1947 to ask the Foreign Ministry what to do. | "居民向兩廣特派員郭德華求援。郭氏乃於十二月四日晉京向外部請示辦法" (the residents asked the Liang-Kuang Special Commissioner 郭德華 for help; on 4 December he went to the capital to ask the Foreign Ministry for instructions) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 11, p. 1, 社論 九龍城事件 (clipping xhrb_article0892, part 1), middle columns | After talks and the protests of the villagers, the Hong Kong Government put off its two-week limit. The Chinese commissioner's protest got no firm answer, and the events of the 12th followed. | "乃使港府限令兩週之期延擱" (thus the Hong Kong Government's two-week limit was put off) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 11, p. 1, 社論 九龍城事件 (clipping xhrb_article0892, part 1), columns 4-2 from the left | The editorial claims that the 1898 lease kept jurisdiction inside Kowloon City for China, and quotes its clause that the Chinese officials in the City may continue their duties. | 「所有現在九龍城內駐劄中國官員，仍可在城內各司其事」 ("all the Chinese officials now stationed in Kowloon City may continue to perform their duties within the city") |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, vol. 26 no. 11, p. 1, 社論 九龍城事件 (clipping xhrb_article0892, part 1), last column | The editorial says that the Kowloon residents had fought for months for their land and for national sovereignty, and now shed blood for it. | "九龍居民月來為其田地，為國家主權，不惜力爭，今日且以流血為保衛國家主權而鬥爭矣" (for months the Kowloon residents have fought hard for their land and national sovereignty; today they even shed blood in the struggle) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, p. 2 (clipping xhrb_article0892, part 2), editorial end | The editorial warns Hong Kong of the memory of the Canton-Hong Kong general strike and names the forced building of the 屏山 airfield as a recent case like the Kowloon City affair. | "則省港大罷工之餘怖，想港方早已嘗透個中滋味；年前屏山機場之強築，今之九龍城事件" (the lingering fear of the Canton-Hong Kong general strike, Hong Kong has surely tasted; the forced building of the Ping Shan airfield some years ago, and now the Kowloon City affair) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, p. 2 (clipping xhrb_article0892, part 2), news item 港警再拆居民茅屋, col. 1-3 | A radio news item of 13 Jan (Chungking) on the same page says that on 12 Jan 1948 Hong Kong police used force against the people living in temporary straw huts in the old City. After their wooden houses were pulled down, the residents had put up these huts against wind and rain. | "（木屋被拆，居民乃搭臨時茅屋，以蔽風雨）" ((after their wooden houses were pulled down, the residents put up temporary straw huts for shelter from wind and rain)) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, p. 2 (clipping xhrb_article0892, part 2), news item 港警再拆居民茅屋, col. 3-5 | The radio item says that the police used tear gas and rifles to force the people in the straw huts to move, and that the official count of injured residents was six. | "曾利用催淚彈及步槍，對付草屋中之我同胞，強迫遷移。據官方調查，昨日我居民受傷者有六人" (used tear gas and rifles against our compatriots in the straw huts, forcing them to move; by official count, six of our residents were injured yesterday) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, p. 2 (clipping xhrb_article0892, part 2), news item 港警再拆居民茅屋, col. 6-9 | Foreign Minister 王 called in the British ambassador 史替文生 and asked that London order the Governor to stop all forcible action in the City during the talks and send no more police in. The ambassador agreed to cable London at once. | "對九龍城終止一切強制行動，並不得再派警察進入該城" (stop all forcible action in Kowloon City and send no more police into the city) |
| Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, p. 2 (clipping xhrb_article0892, part 2), news item 港警再拆居民茅屋, col. 10-12 | The item says that the Foreign Ministry protested to the British embassy on the second day of the forced demolitions, and protested in writing to the British government on 8 Jan 1948. | "並在本月八日，以書面向英國政府提出抗議" (and on the 8th of this month protested in writing to the British government) |

- **Leads:**
  - The Hong Kong Government notice of 27 Nov 1947 to the Kowloon City residents, which cited Ordinance No. 40 of 1932, s. 9(1), and set a limit of two weeks (Shing Wah Daily News editorial, 15 Jan 1948, p. 1).
  - The Chinese Foreign Ministry's written protest to the British government of 8 Jan 1948 (Shing Wah Daily News, 15 Jan 1948, p. 2).
### lead-l0475-1: 九龍城平靜無事 寶安縣長出現 (headline as OCR'd in the archive.org headline list; the last characters are uncertain)

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-24; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-25.
- **Citation:** Kung Sheung Evening News (工商晚報), 20 Jan 1948: 〈九龍城平靜無事，寶安縣長出現馬場〉 (short item); 〈九龍城居民聯會健全組織 重新登記露宿人數縝密管理賑欵分配〉 (專訊); 〈官方否認限期搬遷〉; 〈居民一名一度被拘〉; 〈朱沛唐在獄中情形〉; archive.org leaf n3, https://archive.org/details/NPKSE19480120/page/n3
- **Class:** primary (A Hong Kong evening paper's reports of the time (events of 19 Jan 1948). It is press, not an official record: it belongs in primary-sources/hk-chinese-press-1947-1984 (same tree), as the plan's note says.)
- **Date of the record:** 1948-01-20
- **Files:**
  - `lead-l0475-1-NPKSE19480120-c0cad6bd.pdf` (in `local/`, not in git): Whole issue, image PDF (24.1 MB); https://archive.org/download/NPKSE19480120/NPKSE19480120.pdf; 24144408 bytes; SHA-256 `58a1a3a54cffb1978980721c40f71d87788cb3f13df4cfbb9f3c2621e6049a47`; licence: No rights or licence field in the archive.org metadata (private upload in collection china-mail, probably of HKPL scans); the newspaper text is the copyright of its publisher (https://archive.org/details/NPKSE19480120)
  - `lead-l0475-1-n3-8898b8a7.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-size page image of leaf n3, with all the City items; https://archive.org/download/NPKSE19480120/page/n3.jpg; 5988617 bytes; SHA-256 `75297f90455846a5fa785f4958ee834f5c343551380793e72a396424322c3a24`; licence: No rights or licence field in the archive.org metadata (private upload in collection china-mail, probably of HKPL scans); the newspaper text is the copyright of its publisher (https://archive.org/details/NPKSE19480120)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, column of short items (x-separated), headline line | A one-line item in a column of short notes says that Kowloon City was quiet and that the Bao'an County (寶安縣) magistrate appeared at the racecourse. | "九龍城平靜無事，寶安縣長出現馬場。" (Kowloon City quiet; the Bao'an County magistrate appears at the racecourse.) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, short item, col. 1-6 | The note says that the rumours made 19 Jan the most serious day, and that reporters went to the City early and waited, but nothing special happened all day. | "結果，一天內沒有什麼特殊事情發生" (in the end, nothing special happened all day) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, short item, col. 6-9 | The Bao'an County magistrate, 王啟後, appeared at the racecourse at about 2 pm. The note jokes that after days of work on the Kowloon City affair he had earned a rest. | "寶安縣縣長王啟後，于下午二時許在馬塲出現" (the Bao'an County magistrate 王啟後 appeared at the racecourse at about 2 pm) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 九龍城居民聯會健全組織 (專訊), col. 1-4 | The main report says that Kowloon City was quiet all day on 19 Jan 1948 and the residents' tension eased. Few residents came back into the City after they dispersed in the early morning. | "九龍城昨日整日平靜無事，居民之緊張心情已稍告和緩" (Kowloon City was entirely quiet all day yesterday; the residents' tension has eased a little) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 九龍城居民聯會健全組織, col. 5-9 | The night before, the residents had word that the police would act at 4 am on 19 Jan. The representatives met at once and sent people to report to Bao'an County, to Canton and to the Hong Kong office of the Special Commissioner for Foreign Affairs. | "謂警方將於昨日凌晨四時，採取行動" (saying that the police would take action at 4 am yesterday) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 九龍城居民聯會健全組織, col. 10-14 | Some residents argued all night that the several thousand dollars of relief money from the public, kept by the old resident 曾生, must be shared out at once. More than 100 people gathered and at about 1 am went to 曾生's house on South Wall Road (城南道). | "頓聚集逾百人，於深夜一時許，直至城南道曾生住宅" (at once over a hundred gathered, and at about 1 am went straight to 曾生's house on 城南道) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 九龍城居民聯會健全組織, col. 14-16 | 曾生 handed over HK$5,662. 劉子良 and 劉茂泉 shared it out in public at HK$7 per person. | "曾生乃交出港幣五千六百零六十二元，交劉子良、劉茂泉當衆分配，每人七元" (曾生 then handed over HK$5,662, and gave it to 劉子良 and 劉茂泉, who shared it out in public, seven dollars each) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 九龍城居民聯會健全組織, col. 16-19 | Some said it was not enough, and a further HK$1,160 was drawn. HK$470 was left over and kept by 劉子良; by the afternoon it had not gone back to 曾生. | "再提一千一百六十元，則又剩餘四百七十元，此欵暫存在劉子良處" (another 1,160 dollars was drawn, leaving 470 dollars over, which is kept for now by 劉子良) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 九龍城居民聯會健全組織, col. 20-22 | The share of the relief money and goods, and the count of households and persons, had caused disputes among the residents. | "居民間對賑欵賑品之分配，及戶口人數之數字，曾引起爭端" (among the residents, the division of relief money and goods, and the figures of households and persons, had caused disputes) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 九龍城居民聯會健全組織, second tier, col. 1-4 | At 9 pm on 19 Jan the residents' association met to rearrange its staff and duties, the keeping of relief money and the sharing of relief goods, to make the association sounder. | "對今後居民聯會人事、職權分配，及今後賑欵保管、各方救濟粮物分配工作，研究調整方法" (studied how to adjust the association's staff and duties, and the keeping of relief money and the sharing of relief grain and goods from all sides) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 九龍城居民聯會健全組織, second tier, col. 5-8 | The meeting decided to count again the people sleeping in the open and to give one goods coupon to each person. Only those still sleeping in the City could have them. | "並決議重新檢查露宿者之人數，改發每人一張之領物券" (and resolved to recheck the number of people sleeping in the open, and to issue instead one goods coupon per person) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 九龍城居民聯會健全組織, second tier, col. 8-10 | Some residents proposed to ask the Bao'an County government to send an official to guide them. | "一部居民更建議請寶安縣政府派員指導" (some residents further proposed asking the Bao'an County government to send an official to guide them) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 九龍城居民聯會健全組織, second tier, col. 10-15 | At about 9 am on 19 Jan representatives came with registers and receipts and took in the relief goods sent by the public. Many curious visitors came and went all day. | "不少富有好奇心之遊人，擬能目覩此舉動一時事件之新發展，往來者終日絡繹不絕" (many curious sightseers, hoping to see the latest turn of this event, came and went all day without end) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 九龍城居民聯會健全組織, second tier, col. 15-19 | At 2 pm there was a rumour that police were massing at a police station, and at 4 pm a rumour that British troops would come out with the police. Both proved false. | "下午四時，更謂有英軍協同警察出動" (at 4 pm it was further said that British troops would come out together with the police) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 官方否認限期搬遷 | The Hong Kong Government Public Relations Officer said that the report that a European policeman had told the residents to leave within a set time was untrue. | "港府新聞處長昨宣稱：關於外傳謂有西籍警員曾通知" (the Hong Kong Government Public Relations Officer stated yesterday: as to the outside report that a European policeman had told ...) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 官方否認限期搬遷, end | Of the City residents in hospital since 12 Jan, three were healed and discharged, two more would leave soon, and the one who had surgery was out of danger. | "居民三人，業經痊愈出院，另兩人日內亦可出院" (three residents have healed and left hospital; two more can also leave within days) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 居民一名一度被拘 | A resident, 羅策古, got his share of the relief money, left the City at about 3 am on 19 Jan and vanished. His wife asked at the police station and found that he had been arrested. | "有居民羅策古一名，於領得賑欵後，離城外出，即告失踪" (a resident, 羅策古, after receiving relief money, left the city and at once went missing) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 居民一名一度被拘 | The residents' association reported the arrest to the Hong Kong office of the Foreign Ministry. 羅策古 was released on the afternoon of 19 Jan. The evening item breaks off at the words 查羅氏被拘之原因 (the cause of the arrest); the daily of the same day gives the cause (lead-l0475-3). | "羅氏經於昨（十九）日下午釋出" (Mr 羅 was released yesterday (the 19th) afternoon) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 朱沛唐在獄中情形 | The wife of the jailed representative 朱沛唐, 李惠珍, said that 朱 and 劉 had been moved to the prison at 赤柱, and that she could visit only the next week. | "朱劉二人已移押赤柱" (朱 and 劉 have been moved to custody at 赤柱) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 朱沛唐在獄中情形 | She heard that the two were treated well in prison: each had four blankets, they were in a special room apart from ordinary prisoners, and they bought their three meals a day. | "曾每人發給毛氈四張" (each was given four blankets) |
| NPKSE19480120, leaf n3, 朱沛唐在獄中情形, end | On the appeal, 李惠珍 said that the residents' association was dealing with it. The item breaks off here on the page. The daily of the same day gives the rest, with the barrister 陳丕士 (lead-l0475-3). | "至上訴問題，李妻表示由居聯會" (as to the appeal, Mrs 李 said that the residents' association ... (the item is cut off here)) |

- **Leads:**
  - The English text of the Hong Kong Public Relations Officer's denial of 19 Jan 1948 that police had set a time limit for the Kowloon City residents (INFERRED to be in the English papers of 20 Jan 1948).
### lead-l0475-2: 九龍城事件談判已告中輟 / 曾接警官口頭通知今日遷出昨夜龍城空氣緊張 (headlines as OCR'd in the archive.org headline list)

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-24; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-25.
- **Citation:** Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報), 19 Jan 1948: 〈九龍城事件談判已告中輟〉 (本報特訊, 南京) and 〈穗後援會議决九龍城居民如被迫遷出將採有效行動〉 (本報特訊, 廣州), 第壹張第一頁 (archive.org leaf n0); 〈曾接警官口頭通知今日遷出 昨夜龍城空氣緊張 外特處囑居民安心守法 事態如何發展今日可知〉 (特訊), 第二張第四頁 (leaf n3); https://archive.org/details/NPWK19480119
- **Class:** primary (A Hong Kong daily's reports of the time (events of 15-18 Jan 1948). It is press, not an official record: it belongs in primary-sources/hk-chinese-press-1947-1984 (same tree).)
- **Date of the record:** 1948-01-19
- **Files:**
  - `lead-l0475-2-NPWK19480119-d1cad241.pdf` (in `local/`, not in git): Whole issue, image PDF (4.7 MB); https://archive.org/download/NPWK19480119/NPWK19480119.pdf; 4691790 bytes; SHA-256 `a722263b9ba590696048f14ac565ca9e28fc82f0ff64b1842331473d255fc062`; licence: No rights or licence field in the archive.org metadata (private upload in collection china-mail, probably of HKPL scans); the newspaper text is the copyright of its publisher (https://archive.org/details/NPWK19480119)
  - `lead-l0475-2-n0-b9ba0d0c.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-size page image of leaf n0 (front page), with the two dispatches; https://archive.org/download/NPWK19480119/page/n0.jpg; 5140518 bytes; SHA-256 `b24982d4398e7ed13f7363e6a2c8f0a9d16ce339bb5adacbd93ce60db2680903`; licence: No rights or licence field in the archive.org metadata (private upload in collection china-mail, probably of HKPL scans); the newspaper text is the copyright of its publisher (https://archive.org/details/NPWK19480119)
  - `lead-l0475-2-n3-2fdeaa94.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-size page image of leaf n3 (第二張第四頁), with the City report; https://archive.org/download/NPWK19480119/page/n3.jpg; 5365618 bytes; SHA-256 `f3af933ef389e13ef92f28d4bfac45586f9301f9588bdbd7cc9102983df12253`; licence: No rights or licence field in the archive.org metadata (private upload in collection china-mail, probably of HKPL scans); the newspaper text is the copyright of its publisher (https://archive.org/details/NPWK19480119)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPWK19480119, leaf n0 (printed 第壹張第一頁), 九龍城事件談判已告中輟, col. 1-2 | From Nanking: Foreign Minister 王 and the British ambassador 施諦文 talked on 15 and 16 Jan 1948 and had reached a fair agreement on the Kowloon City affair. | "王外長與施諦文大使十五十六兩日迭經磋商後，原已獲得相當協議" (after repeated talks on the 15th and 16th, Foreign Minister 王 and Ambassador 施諦文 had reached a fair agreement) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n0 (printed 第壹張第一頁), 九龍城事件談判已告中輟, col. 2-4 | The agreement had three points. Britain would order Hong Kong to stop all forcible action. Hong Kong would house the residents of the demolished area for the time being, and the disputed ground would become a park. Hong Kong would pay for the losses and medical costs of those harmed by the police's forcible action. | "拆屋區居民暫由港方供給住屋，而將成問題之處闢作公園" (the residents of the demolition area to be housed for now by Hong Kong, and the place in dispute to be made into a park) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n0 (printed 第壹張第一頁), 九龍城事件談判已告中輟, col. 4-6 | After the 沙面 incident in Canton on the afternoon of 16 Jan, the British ambassador reserved all rights and the talks broke off. On 17 Jan the Foreign Ministry and the British embassy both kept silent. (A few worn characters between 英使 and 保留 are not read.) | "乃於十六日午後發生沙面事件，英使……保留一切權利，談判於是中輟" (then on the afternoon of the 16th the 沙面 incident occurred; the British ambassador ... reserved all rights, and the talks broke off) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n0 (printed 第壹張第一頁), 九龍城事件談判已告中輟, second dispatch | A second dispatch from Nanking says that the talks were stopped until the details of the Canton incident were known. The ministry sent 潘漢奎 to Canton to investigate. Up to 6 pm on 18 Jan the Foreign Ministry had no document from the British side. | "迄十八日下午六時外部未接獲英方任何文件" (up to 6 pm on the 18th the Foreign Ministry had not received any document from the British side) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n0 (printed 第壹張第一頁), 穗後援會議决九龍城居民如被迫遷出將採有效行動, col. 1-2 | From Canton: people there were watching to see whether Hong Kong would again force the City residents out on 19 Jan. | "此間各界正密切注意十九日港府是否再實行強迫九龍城居民遷出" (all circles here are closely watching whether on the 19th the Hong Kong Government will again forcibly move the Kowloon City residents out) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n0 (printed 第壹張第一頁), 穗後援會議决九龍城居民如被迫遷出將採有效行動, col. 3-6 | INFERRED: The Canton foreign-affairs support committee called an emergency meeting to raise funds on a large scale, and planned meetings on 20 Jan to decide on "practical action". (The character 籌 before 欵 is worn: INFERRED.) | "大規模籌欵" (large-scale fund-raising) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n3 (printed 第二張第四頁), article 曾接警官口頭通知今日遷出 昨夜龍城空氣緊張, headline | The headline on p. 4 says that the residents had an oral notice from a police officer to move out on 19 Jan, and that the City was tense on the night of 18 Jan. | "曾接警官口頭通知今日遷出 昨夜龍城空氣緊張" (had an oral notice from a police officer to move out today; the atmosphere in Kowloon City tense last night) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n3 (printed 第二張第四頁), article 曾接警官口頭通知今日遷出 昨夜龍城空氣緊張, col. 1-3 | At 2.30 pm on 18 Jan the residents' association sent its representative 胡金 to the office of the Special Commissioner for Foreign Affairs, to ask what to do. On 13 Jan a European police officer had told the residents orally to leave by 19 Jan. | "因本月十三日居民曾接某西警官口頭通知，限於今（十九）日遷離他往" (because on the 13th of this month the residents had an oral notice from a European police officer to leave by today (the 19th)) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n3 (printed 第二張第四頁), article 曾接警官口頭通知今日遷出 昨夜龍城空氣緊張, col. 3-5 | The commissioner 郭德華 had no time; his secretary 區 received 胡金. He said that the central government's instructions had not yet come, and told everyone to stay calm, obey the law and wait for its orders. | "中央之指示，仍未頒到，着各人安心守法，靜候中央命令" (the central government's instructions have not yet arrived; everyone is to stay calm, obey the law, and wait for the central government's orders) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n3 (printed 第二張第四頁), article 曾接警官口頭通知今日遷出 昨夜龍城空氣緊張, col. 5-6 | That night the association called an emergency meeting, and the City was tense. | "故昨夜居民會深夜召開臨時緊急會議" (thus late last night the residents' association called a special emergency meeting) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n3 (printed 第二張第四頁), article 曾接警官口頭通知今日遷出 昨夜龍城空氣緊張, col. 6-9 | A rumour of a new incident on the night of 17 Jan was false. At about midnight a European police sergeant on night patrol came with two Chinese constables to the association and told the staff to go to bed. The staff answered that they were used to going to bed late. | "該處辦事人答以習慣遲眠，如疲倦自會睡覺" (the office staff answered that they were used to sleeping late, and would sleep when tired) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n3 (printed 第二張第四頁), article 曾接警官口頭通知今日遷出 昨夜龍城空氣緊張, relief paragraph, col. 1-2 | The Bao'an magistrate 王啟後 had come from Canton with 徐鑫濬 of the social affairs bureau and others to give out relief. The badly injured 梁鵬 and 黎鳳英, and the jailed 朱沛唐 and 劉毅夫, got CN$5,000,000 each. | "每人國幣五百萬元" (five million dollars of national currency each) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n3 (printed 第二張第四頁), article 曾接警官口頭通知今日遷出 昨夜龍城空氣緊張, relief paragraph, col. 2-3 | INFERRED: The lightly injured 張忠武, 劉三妹, a person whose name ends in 日 (the first character is worn; perhaps 孫日: INFERRED) and 張衆林 got CN$2,000,000 each. (The Toronto report, lead-l0013-2, lists 張忠武 and 孫日 among the six badly hurt.) | "另輕傷之張忠武劉三妹" (and the lightly injured 張忠武, 劉三妹 ...) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n3 (printed 第二張第四頁), article 曾接警官口頭通知今日遷出 昨夜龍城空氣緊張, relief paragraph, col. 3-5 | On 18 Jan the officials went on paying the other residents whose homes were pulled down: CN$200,000 each, on a cloth goods certificate issued by the residents' association. About 797 people received it. | "每人國幣二十萬元，領取者持居民會發給之布製領物證。查領取者約七百九十七人" (two hundred thousand dollars of national currency each; the receivers held cloth goods certificates issued by the residents' association; about 797 received it) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n3 (printed 第二張第四頁), article 曾接警官口頭通知今日遷出 昨夜龍城空氣緊張, relief paragraph, col. 5-6 | The money left over, CN$312,200,000, was to be spent entirely on rice for the residents. | "餘欵三億一千二百二十萬元，預備完全購米散發" (the remaining money, 312,200,000 dollars, is to be used entirely to buy rice for distribution) |
| NPWK19480119, leaf n3 (printed 第二張第四頁), article 曾接警官口頭通知今日遷出 昨夜龍城空氣緊張, relief paragraph, col. 6-8 | INFERRED: The rice shop agreed a price of HK$53 a picul (担) for 21,000 catties of rice. The exchange rate was not yet settled, and the shop did not have enough rice, thus the rice was to come on 19 Jan and then be given out. (The character before 担 is worn; 每 is INFERRED.) | "買米二萬一千斤，但比率未定妥，且該米店存米不足" (to buy 21,000 catties of rice, but the exchange rate is not settled, and the rice shop has not enough in stock) |
### lead-l0475-3: 香港工商日報 1948-01-20 (headline list names 九龍城)

- **Status:** Read on 2026-11-24; a checker checked a sample of the facts on 2026-11-25.
- **Citation:** Kung Sheung Daily News (香港工商日報), 20 Jan 1948, report on Kowloon City of 19 Jan 1948 (headlines 九龍城昨日平靜無事 / 官方否認 / 居民聯會 ... 縝密管理賑欵物品分配; 專訊), with a photo by 林季廉, archive.org leaf n4, https://archive.org/details/NPKS19480120/page/n4
- **Class:** primary (A Hong Kong daily's report of the time (events of 19 Jan 1948). It is press, not an official record: it belongs in primary-sources/hk-chinese-press-1947-1984 (same tree).)
- **Date of the record:** 1948-01-20
- **Files:**
  - `lead-l0475-3-NPKS19480120-d0b35060.pdf` (in `local/`, not in git): Whole issue, image PDF (4.1 MB); https://archive.org/download/NPKS19480120/NPKS19480120.pdf; 4132647 bytes; SHA-256 `d7c9be88e83e768bd4e1ac462c839780ad81a657e3d4d9715763696e22cb2030`; licence: No rights or licence field in the archive.org metadata (private upload in collection china-mail, probably of HKPL scans); the newspaper text is the copyright of its publisher (https://archive.org/details/NPKS19480120)
  - `lead-l0475-3-n4-a3646b0d.jpg` (in `local/`, not in git): Full-size page image of leaf n4, with the City report and photo; https://archive.org/download/NPKS19480120/page/n4.jpg; 5254311 bytes; SHA-256 `569206ac0d6d9f345bd92e0e56f98131b99111952aef85bce23f933d2968dc00`; licence: No rights or licence field in the archive.org metadata (private upload in collection china-mail, probably of HKPL scans); the newspaper text is the copyright of its publisher (https://archive.org/details/NPKS19480120)

| Page | Fact | Words in the source |
|---|---|---|
| NPKS19480120, leaf n4, opening (專訊) | The Hong Kong Public Relations Officer said that the outside report, that European police had told the walled city residents to leave within one week, was quite untrue. | "關於外傳謂有西籍警員曾通知九龍砦城居民限期一星期離開該處之說，全屬子虛" (the outside report that European police had told the residents of the Kowloon walled city to leave within one week is entirely false) |
| NPKS19480120, leaf n4, opening, second paragraph | From midnight on 18 Jan for 24 hours the residents were more tense than at any time since the City question began. They believed that the British police's oral warning of a week before had just run out. (The print has a stray 日 after 英警.) | "因彼等認為根據英警日一星期前之口頭警告，適已到期也" (because they believed that, by the oral warning of the British police a week before, the time had just expired) |
| NPKS19480120, leaf n4, opening, second paragraph | Against the residents' fears, the armed police still kept watch on the hillside of the City, but there was no sign of action. Up to midnight on 19 Jan nothing happened. | "雖仍繼續在山腰警戒，但並無若干動靜，截至深夜十二時止，尚未有任何事故發生" (though they still kept guard on the hillside, there was no movement; up to midnight nothing at all had happened) |
| NPKS19480120, leaf n4, block x1380-1620, col. 2 | After the relief money was shared out at about 3 am, the residents sleeping in the open picked up their bedding and things and left. | "昨日凌晨三時許，賑欵分發完畢之後，露宿之居民，即陸續檢拾舖蓋及用具離去" (at about 3 am yesterday, after the relief money was all given out, the residents sleeping in the open picked up their bedding and things and left one after another) |
| NPKS19480120, leaf n4, block x1380-1620, col. 3-4 | INFERRED: When the reporter came at 6 am, the four big tents were empty. In the ten-odd crude wooden enclosures (木柵) there were only about ten adult men on guard (the figure 十 is INFERRED from a worn character). | "四個大營幕內，空無一人，其他十餘座簡陋之木柵內" (in the four big tents there was nobody; in the other ten-odd crude wooden enclosures ...) |
| NPKS19480120, leaf n4, block x1380-1620, col. 4-5 | At the flag-raising at about 6 am only some ten people were there, fewer than the reporters sent by the papers. | "舉行升旗禮時，亦只得寥寥十數人，人數尚較各報社派往採訪之記者為少" (at the flag-raising ceremony there were only ten-odd people, fewer than the reporters sent by the papers) |
| NPKS19480120, leaf n4, block x1380-1620, col. 5-7 | At about 9 am representatives came with registers and receipts and took in the relief goods. Curious visitors came and went all day. | "始有代表攜同慰問登記表冊、收據等到塲，點收各界送來之賑品" (only then did representatives come with relief registers, receipts and so on, and check in the relief goods sent by all circles) |
| NPKS19480120, leaf n4, block x1720-1990 | The daily also tells of the night share-out of the relief money, by 劉茂泉 and others, as in the evening paper (lead-l0475-1). | "劉茂泉當衆分配" (劉茂泉 shared it out in public) |
| NPKS19480120, leaf n4, photo caption | The report has a photo of the City after the residents left, by the paper's photographer 林季廉. | "居民疏散後之九龍城（本報林季廉攝）" (Kowloon City after the residents dispersed (photo by our 林季廉)) |
| NPKS19480120, leaf n4, left block of the report (items on the arrest and on 朱沛唐) | The daily gives the cause of the arrest of the resident 羅氏 (羅策古 in the evening paper): he was on the street late at night with money on him, and the police suspected him. When they learned the facts they let him go on the afternoon of 19 Jan. | "係因深夜出現在街道上，且身懷欵項，致引起警察懷疑，但經查悉真相後，即予釋放云" (it was because he was out on the street late at night with money on him, which made the police suspicious; but after they learned the truth they released him) |
| NPKS19480120, leaf n4, left block of the report (items on the arrest and on 朱沛唐) | On the appeal of the two jailed representatives, 李惠珍 said that the residents' association was in charge. The residents' representatives said that the barrister 陳丕士 was already working on it with the Special Commissioner 郭德華. | "但居民代表則謂，已由陳丕士大律師與外交特派員郭德華聯絡進行" (but the residents' representatives said that the barrister 陳丕士 was already in touch with the Special Commissioner for Foreign Affairs 郭德華 and proceeding) |

- **Creator:** Central Daily News (中央日報), Nanjing; dispatches of Central News Agency (中央社)
- **Date:** 1948-01-17
- **Kind:** press
- **Class:** primary (A newspaper of the day after the events; it prints the order of the Executive Yuan of 16 Jan 1948 in the words of the government news agency.)
- **Value:** low

- **What it gives (resolver's note, not checked):** The Chinese Government's answer of 16 Jan 1948 to the burning of the British Consulate-General at Canton: the Executive Yuan's order to punish the rioters and protect British consulates and nationals, with regret for the incident; and the Canton march against the 'Kowloon affair' (the January 1948 evictions from the City).
